# PROJECT_CONTEXT.md

## What ATO is
A self-based alternative to Costar-style astrology apps. Instead of generic daily content, it uses a multi-axis personality/behavior framework to generate accurate, personalized guidance. Goal: help users understand themselves and how they interact with others (e.g. why an extrovert might overwhelm an introvert), and nudge them toward growth and new experiences.

**Confirmed 16 axes** (`TRAIT_AXES` in `src/lib/traits.ts:15`): openness, conscientiousness, extraversion, agreeableness, steadiness, attachment_anxiety, attachment_avoidance, conflict_assertiveness, conflict_cooperativeness, autonomy, competence, relatedness, growth_mindset, locus_of_control, self_efficacy, playfulness.

Pointer only below this. Live status lives in the three tracked docs — keep them in sync, commit together, and `git push` immediately.

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `docs/NOW.md` | What's shipped, latest OTA, what's left, next 15 min |
| `docs/ME.md` | Product + roster + live AI/model |
| `docs/MAP.md` | Every file, one line |
| `docs/FLOWS.md` | Data flows with file:line |
| `docs/GOTCHAS.md` | Known traps — read before editing an area |
| `docs/archive/OLD_PLAN.md` | Old plan — reference only, suggestions not rules |
| `docs/BUSINESS.md` | Legal / brand / cost |

Expo SDK **54** in this repo (`CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md` point at the v54 docs).

Do not commit `.env.local` or API keys. Do not change dependencies, schemas, auth, env config, or secrets without asking emci first.

## House conventions

- **Every response — not just a final "done" report — must end with an explicit, unmissable "what's needed from emci / what happens next" line.** (Sep 13, 2026, emci explicit: "when code outputs I want it to tell me whats needed from me or whats next?? ... it just stops there.") Never end a substantive turn on just a status summary, buried "Next action:" line, or a vague "otherwise this is a good stopping point." Concretely:
  - If nothing is needed and work continues automatically: say so plainly ("Nothing needed — continuing to the next screen now.").
  - If emci needs to decide something: ask it as a direct question, not a buried footnote.
  - If a new chat is the right move (per the checkpoint rule below): say so explicitly AND give the exact copy-paste prompt for the new chat, every time — don't assume emci remembers the convention or will infer it from "this is a good /clear point."
  - This applies in every session working on this repo, not just the one where this was said — it's a standing rule, not a one-off preference.
- **Dev-only/internal tooling changes** (Dev Lab, test presets, anything not on a production user path): do NOT re-run typecheck/lint/`check:ota-gate` after every intermediate edit. Batch all edits for the task and stop once they're done — no automatic full-gate run, no reviewer round-trip loop. Emci tests these manually on device.
- **Add a manual-verification hook on an existing dev surface** whenever a fix or feature would benefit from it and a dev-only path already exists (Dev Lab, dev-test-user presets, etc.) — a quick toggle/button/preset there instead of (or alongside) an automated check, so emci can tap and confirm it without another investigation round. Applies anywhere a dev surface reasonably fits, not just onboarding/intake.
- **Do not run the full gate** (typecheck/lint/`check:ota-gate`) automatically before commit/push. Only run it when emci explicitly asks. If a change looks risky enough to warrant it, say so and recommend it — don't run it unprompted.
- **This is a pre-production sandbox — zero live users, not publicly deployed.** Emci's explicit, broadened call (Sep 13, 2026): "we are literally not on production yet this whole thing is a sandbox... no issues with passwords being strong or anything... we need everything working first before we think about security." Concretely, this means:
  - **Do not treat credential/password/secret rotation, strength, or reuse as a blocker.** Weak/memorable/shared values are fine for now and get fixed as one pass right before public launch (pre-launch re-gating checklist, items 2,3,4,6,7,8,9,11,12).
  - **Test data and test accounts (dev-test user, throwaway signups, etc.) can be freely deleted, edited, or reset** — including via direct Supabase Auth/dashboard actions — without treating it as a risky/destructive action requiring extra caution. There is nothing real to lose.
  - **Security hardening, RLS tightening, rate-limiting, secret rotation — all of it is explicitly deferred** until pre-launch prep. Don't propose it, don't block progress on it, don't pause to ask about it. Log it (GOTCHAS.md / the re-gating checklist / NOW.md Left) and move on.
  - **Priority right now is core functionality actually working** — real bugs, broken flows, missing features. If something fails or doesn't take (like a rotation attempt), don't retry it inline — spin it off (`spawn_task`) and keep moving on core work.
  - This does NOT relax: don't touch production data/config for OTHER projects, don't act destructively outside this repo's own Supabase project, and the actual pre-launch security pass still has to happen and be verified before `signup_mode` goes public — deferred is not skipped.
- **Gate + OTA discipline during the core-fix effort (Sep 13, 2026, emci explicit, strengthened):** the full `check:ota-gate` (74 checks) and `npm run ota:publish` are both SLOW and are NOT part of the default per-fix loop anymore. Per change: `npm run typecheck` + eslint on the touched file(s) + the ONE most relevant `check:*` script if an obvious one exists — nothing more. **Do not publish an OTA per fix.** Batch fixes; publish/run the full gate only at a real milestone emci actually asks for (e.g. "publish what we have" or the end of a full screen-pass phase) — not reflexively after every commit. This supersedes the earlier version of this rule, which was still being run too often in practice.
- **Checking cadence — ONE gate, ONE reviewer, at the end (Sep 14, 2026, emci explicit; supersedes the two bullets above wherever they conflict).** Do NOT run the full gate, individual `check:*` scripts, or the `reviewer` subagent after each file, sub-step, or task card. **Write the entire change first.** Then run `npm run check:ota-gate` and the reviewer **once**, at the very end, right before committing. If something fails: fix it once, re-run that once to confirm, and stop — do not loop back into repeated full-gate or repeated reviewer passes. This is a standing working agreement that carries forward to every task automatically, not a per-task instruction that needs restating. **Why:** the per-card cadence used during T-02/T-E1/T-H1 ran the gate four times and the reviewer twice for one session's work, which is slow and wasteful when the end state is what actually matters.
- **UI/IA scaffold-first approach (Sep 13, 2026, emci's explicit plan — supersedes ad-hoc bug-hunting as the default mode).** Full mechanics:
  1. **Split every fix into "single" (screen-specific) or "shared" (used by 2+ screens) before starting it.** Quick grep/import-check first — don't screen-lock a fix that should be shared.
  2. **Screen-by-screen scaffold pass first**, nav order: Home → Explore → Sage → You → Around → Circle → Categories → Legends → Roll → Public Profile → Dev Tools last. Reference: `docs/screen-map.md` (built earlier this session, catalogs every section). For each screen, confirm what's actually there against the spec and fix single/screen-specific issues. Where something is missing or depends on broken shared code, leave a **visible, grep-able placeholder** — literal tag `[not built yet]` for something never implemented, `[blocked on shared fix]` for something that depends on a shared-code fix — never silently missing. One screen verified (typecheck + targeted check) before moving to the next, so nothing cascades.
  3. **Shared-code fix pass second**, as its own isolated batch: go through everything tagged `[blocked on shared fix]`, fix each shared piece once. Verification differs by kind: pure logic/server code (Edge Functions) can be fully verified in isolation (a script, a live-check) before touching any screen; visual/UI shared components need one look on a real representative screen after the code-level fix, since code review alone can't confirm they render right. When a shared fix un-placeholders multiple screens at once, glance at each affected screen once — "shared" doesn't guarantee identical behavior everywhere, screens can pass it different data.
  4. **Tag placeholders by kind**, not just presence/absence — `[not built yet]` (needs building, net-new) vs `[blocked on shared fix]` (exists, needs a shared-code fix) are different follow-up work and should stay distinguishable.
  5. **Do not trust old planning docs' "done" claims at face value** (`CORE_LOOP_REDESIGN_PLAN.md`, `docs/archive/OLD_PLAN.md`, stretches of this file's own Decisions log) — verify against current live/rendered state. Drift has repeatedly turned out to be real this session in both directions (things marked done that weren't verified, and things marked open that were already fixed).
  6. **Closing signal:** once every screen is scaffolded (step 2) and every shared item is fixed (step 3), THAT is when the full `check:ota-gate` + an actual device pass happens — once, not per-fix. That's the "we're actually done" checkpoint, not a milestone along the way.
- **Edit vs. rewrite (Sep 13, 2026, emci explicit):** don't be precious about preserving or carefully patching code that's already confirmed broken — recreate/replace it outright rather than spending tokens on a surgical edit of something not working. The instinct to "reuse existing code" only applies when the existing code actually works or needs a genuinely small tweak — don't force-reuse or delicately edit around something broken just to minimize the diff.
- **New-chat checkpoints for this effort:** this planning session (field-inventory → judgment-pass → several build rounds → this full replan) is long — **start the actual screen-by-screen execution in a fresh chat now**, pointed at this file + `docs/screen-map.md` (both carry everything needed to start cold). Within the execution phase itself, `/clear` again at each natural phase boundary: after the foundational health check, after the screen-by-screen pass finishes, and before the shared-code fix pass starts — three phases, three chats, not one continuous session for the whole effort. Don't wait for a session to feel sluggish before clearing; clear at the phase boundary on principle.
- **Model:** stay on Sonnet 5 for the screen-by-screen scaffold/fix work — it's volume building, not a hard architecture call. Per the existing retry rules, Opus (`architect`) only enters automatically after a specific task has failed twice with different hypotheses — don't pre-emptively switch for the whole effort.

The real trap found instead: the code default `DEFAULT_MODELS.gemini` was `gemini-2.5-flash`, which Google now answers with **404 "no longer available to new users"** — so any build where `EXPO_PUBLIC_GEMINI_MODEL` is unset fails on every call regardless of quota. Default switched to `gemini-3.7-flash`. Whether the EAS production env sets `EXPO_PUBLIC_GEMINI_MODEL` could not be read from this session (`eas env:list` failed non-interactively). **Superseded Sep 3, 2026:** the client no longer reads any `EXPO_PUBLIC_*_MODEL` or `*_API_KEY` (model + key are decided inside `ai-generate`), so those EAS vars are inert — delete them when next logged in to EAS (`docs/NOW.md` Left). Every Gemini failure (not only quota) now falls back once to DeepSeek.

**Open for emci (see ISOLATION_PLAN §7.6):** the device pass + OTA publish; the
rest of the deletion sweep (~20 unreachable modules whose logic a check script
still covers — deleting them deletes that coverage); the tab bar reads Home /
Explore / You / More, so **Questions is not a visible tab** (reachable from More
and Home's main button) because parked Sage still occupies a pinned slot; and
`logCrisisFlag` has had zero callers since before this work, so `crisisToday` is
always false and the CrisisCard kept Active per §0 decision 3 can never render.

**Two sequencing rules found in the 2026-09-15 red-team pass (now in ISOLATION_PLAN §5.1-5.3):**
park `RollHistoryFold` per call site, never wholesale, or Card 4 silently changes Explore;
ship Cards 3+4 together, or Story spends tokens that can no longer be earned; and never add
Home/Explore/Questions to `PARKED_SCREENS` — that check assumes a whole-file park.

**Token economy — researched 2026-09-15, NOT built, NOT approved.** emci ran the design
research separately. Headline: non-monetary v1, never sell tokens directly, core self-insight
always free, earn from completed reflection (no streak multipliers), append-only `token_ledger`
with `SUM` balance + per-event idempotency key, daily earn cap 40, soft cap 500, no transfers,
ledger cleared on account deletion. Build it **after Cards 1-6**, not before — the old wiring is
dead by then. **Open gap: `Divecore`**, a game emci is building alongside ATO, is meant to feed
tokens and the v1 numbers do not account for it. emci deferred that knowingly; surface it before
any number is locked.

**Honest ceiling:** call-graph isolation is achievable; *type* isolation is not,
because `Me` and `TraitState` stay shared shapes. `lib/me.ts` remains a god-object
that can break every screen at once. Fixing that is a deeper rewrite, out of scope.

  **The rule, stated once so it stops moving: consent gates GENERATION, never
  navigation.** A model call needs a yes; a screen, a route, a Check, or
  anything served from local data does not.

  |---|---|---|

  | Explore packs / `routeExplore` | yes | **YES** |

  | Logging a Check | no | **NO** |
  | Home / Explore / any route | no | **NO** |

  account isn't told to finish for a beat on cold open.

## Pre-launch re-gating checklist (must re-gate or remove before public launch)

These dev/testing conveniences are deliberately un-`__DEV__`-gated (via the
`PRE_LAUNCH_DEV` flag in `src/lib/dev-mode.ts`) so they work over OTA while the
app is invite-only. Before `signup_mode` flips to `public`, each must be
re-gated to `__DEV__` (or removed), and the Metro `PROBE_STUB` re-added:

1. ~~Dev-test-user "Sign in as dev user" button~~ — **removed entirely 2026-09-03**, along with the hardcoded password/auto-login in `dev-test-user.ts`. Dev Tools Hub access post-launch now comes from root, a per-account grant, or the hidden password unlock behind the 7-tap version number (`dev-unlock-gate.tsx` → `dev-unlock` Edge Function → `DEV_UNLOCK_PASSWORD` Supabase secret, session-only, never persisted). The dev-test account's own Supabase password is still in git history — rotate before public launch (deliberately deferred).
2. Legends "test persona" strip (`src/app/(tabs)/legends.tsx` + `applyDevArchetypePreset`).
3. Six `*-lab` screens (`_layout.tsx` guard + each lab's redirect).
4. Home slot/ask overrides (`dev-overrides.ts` + `index.tsx`).
5. ~~Home "dev" links box (`index.tsx`)~~ — **done 2026-09-02, `__DEV__`**.
6. You-tab growth preview (`you.tsx`).
7. You-tab crash/push probes (`you.tsx` + `you-dev-tools.tsx` + `push-test-card.tsx`
   + `sentry-test-card.tsx`) — also re-add the Metro `PROBE_STUB` and invert `check:prod-you`.
8. Dawn dev-trace line (`dawn.tsx`).
9. Dev-lab unconditional dev access (`dev-lab.tsx` `isDev` + `CrisisCardPreview`).
10. ~~Sentry native-crash probe (`sentry.ts`)~~ — **done 2026-09-02, `__DEV__`**.
11. Intake-sweep "draft copy" badge (`intake-sweep.tsx`).
12. **QA override invite code (`public.app_secrets.qa_override_invite_code`, wave42) is
    deliberately guessable** — as of Sep 13, 2026 it is a short dictionary word, chosen
    by emci over a generated high-entropy value ("i dont care who can guess it i can
    switch this later"). **This is the single gate keeping signup closed**, and
    `assert_invite_usable` is **anon-callable with no rate limiting in the schema**, so
    right now anyone who guesses it can create an account. Acceptable only because the
    app is invite-only with no live users and everyone is reset before launch.
    **ROTATE TO A LONG, HIGH-ENTROPY VALUE BEFORE `signup_mode` GOES PUBLIC** —
    `update public.app_secrets set qa_override_invite_code = '...' where id = 1;`
    Same for the dev-test account password and `DEV_UNLOCK_PASSWORD`, both of which were
    set to the same short memorable value on the same date and for the same reason.
    Note: override signups leave `referred_by = null` with no `invite_codes` audit trail,
    so a guessed-code signup is also invisible in the referral chain.
13. **Play free loop (`PLAY_EVERYTHING_FREE` in `src/lib/dev-mode.ts`)** — every hero
    owned on Play load (no Premium). Mirrors `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV`, so it flips off at the
    same re-gate; a public build must not hand out the whole hero roster for free.
14. **`public.play_dev_logs` (wave73)** — non-personal Play dev test results, anon-readable
    so a dev machine can pull them with the public key. Drop the table (or at least the
    anon select + authenticated insert) before `signup_mode` goes public. NOT covered by
    the `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV` flip — this one is a database change.

One flag controls all of these: `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV = true` in `src/lib/dev-mode.ts`. **`npm run check:release-mode` (wired as the EAS `eas-build-post-install` hook) refuses a production build while it is `true`.**

## Decisions log

- 2026-09-16: **Play — hard fence + `library.md` LF, on `play/grove-v0` (no gameplay).** Two deliverables before OTA. **(1) The Play ⇄ app fence.** New `.cursor/rules/play-isolation.mdc` (always-applied rule) + `scripts/check-play-isolation.ts` + `"check:play-isolation"` script, wired into `check:ota-gate` for free (ota-gate runs every `check:*` script not in its EXCLUDED set). The check reads the current changeset (uncommitted → unpushed-vs-origin → last commit) and FAILS if it mixes Play paths (`src/play/**`, `assets/play/**`, `games/grove/**`, the play-art-prep + check-{heroes,creeps,towers} scripts) with non-Play app paths (`src/**` minus play, `assets/**` minus play, `supabase/`, `docs/`), after the allowlist (src/app/play.tsx, library.md, PROJECT_CONTEXT/AGENTS/CLAUDE, .cursor/**, .gitattributes, package.json, ota-gate.ts, the check itself). A task labeled app/Soft/Zen must not stage Play files; a Play task must not stage app files. **(2) `library.md` LF.** The `check:library` gate failure was a Windows `core.autocrlf=true` checkout artifact, not a committed CRLF — `git ls-files --eol` shows the index already LF (`i/lf w/crlf`); the raw `readFileSync` in `library-check.ts:66` reads the CRLF working copy and its `### For Sage\n\n` regex counts 0 blocks. Fix = new `.gitattributes` forcing `src/app/copy/library.md text eol=lf` (durable on any Windows clone) + strip the working copy to LF; **no Sage copy text changed**. `AGENTS.md` + `games/grove/CAST_DROP.md` gained the airport + fence notes (16 heroes, skipped INFERNA/masterpiece_premium, slot masks, premium parked). Commit + push, then `npm run ota:publish`.

- 2026-09-16: **Play merged onto `master` (commit `46cff93`) — game now on top of the app, and OTA blocked by EAS's 1000-asset hard limit.** Per emci ("accept all master updates, add the game on top, push to master"), merged `origin/master` into `play/grove-v0` (82 Play commits onto 147 app commits), resolved three conflicts (`_layout.tsx` voice-lab/play screen, `milestone-toast.tsx` style/hold-timing, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` checklist item 12) keeping BOTH sides' app changes AND the game, then fast-forwarded `origin/master` to the merge commit (`53884fe..46cff93`). **OTA attempted and BLOCKED, not by the gate (green, 85 offline checks) but by EAS Update's hard 1000-assets-per-update-per-platform limit** — the bundle exports **3111 assets** (3193 Play art PNGs: 1618 newly-ingested hero frames + 1575 pre-existing cast/kenney/craftpix/tiles + 89 app icons). `games/` (6158 reference source PNGs) is correctly NOT bundled. This is a platform ceiling with no bypass (staging allows 20k; production is 1000). **Exact next step to receive it on-device: a new native EAS Build** (`npx eas-cli build --platform ios --profile production` — embeds the hero art into the binary, not subject to the OTA asset cap), submit to TestFlight, install, then reserve `eas update` for small changes. Do NOT trim hero art to force an OTA — that would gut the roster the pass was meant to ship. Not device-verified.

- 2026-09-16: **Build #11 (production profile, `e995895b`) failed at the Post-install hook — by design, not a bug.** `eas-build-post-install` runs `scripts/release-mode-check.ts`, which refuses any `production`-profile build while `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV = true` (gate added Sep 2 in `b04cd35`, after binary 10 — so #11 was the first native build to hit it). Not caused by JS changes. **Fix (emci approved, commit `8414dc2`): new `testflight` build profile in `eas.json` (`extends: production` — same env, channel `production`, autoIncrement, ASC app) that the gate does not enforce, so invite-only TestFlight builds keep dev tooling + `PLAY_EVERYTHING_FREE`.** The public launch build MUST still use `--profile production` (with `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV = false`). Command: `npx eas-cli build --platform ios --profile testflight --auto-submit-with-profile production`. Build #12 (`59757530-5eec-4f52-a5b3-34ff9f2d4485`) triggered with auto-submit to TestFlight. **Build #12 FINISHED and the EAS submission reached Apple (2026-09-16 18:44) with no error** — not device-verified.

- 2026-09-17: **Sprite-sheet spike shipped (`83ae4a8`) — the crop approach is proven, the library is NOT converted yet.** Context: the Play merge pushed the bundle to ~3111 exported assets against EAS Update's hard **1000-asset-per-update** cap, so every OTA is blocked and each change needs a native build. Plan of record is: pack the per-frame art into sheets (bundle drops under the cap, OTAs work again), then host the sheets on Supabase Storage (free plan is ample — ~30MB against 1GB storage / 5GB egress) with on-device caching, then a Sprite HQ for assigning actions to roles. **What the spike proves:** `scripts/play-art-pack.ts` packs a hero into one sheet PER ACTION (per-hero sheets would be ~15MB decoded; per-action ~2MB, and only the playing clip stays in memory), rows = directions, cols = frames, 1px gutter against texture bleeding, 4096px cap for Android drivers. archangel: 172 frames → 8 sheets, 1.4MB → 976KB, **all 164 animation frames verified byte-identical to source**. `footAt` is now MEASURED from the alpha bbox (0.7422 vs the 0.7417 hand-tuned) instead of eyeballed. Sheets are additive — the game still draws per-frame art; `src/play/sheet-sprite.tsx` (nested `<Svg viewBox={rect}>`, no ClipPath) and the dev-only Sheet Lab (Play dev kit → Misc) are the only consumers. **Still unproven: how the crop renders on a real device** (crispness of pixel art, remote/file URIs) — that is the next gate before converting 15 more heroes. **Decisions made:** rotation strips are packed in COMPASS order, not alphabetical (alphabetical made the turn jump — caught by emci on the review page); the action catalog keeps EVERY action under its authored name so skills can call one by name; role guesses are proposals cross-checked against `heroes.json` and reported on disagreement, never silently applied (archangel's `Celestial_Drift` vs `Celestial_Rush` for dash is flagged). A review page for emci is published as an Artifact (`https://claude.ai/artifact/3XrBTS6vj3o84eVJNCr4B7`) with a role picker; changing a role there is preview-only today. **Open question for emci:** whether the hero→role mapping should stay in `heroes.json` (change = commit + OTA) or move to Supabase so a Sprite HQ screen can change it live with no build.

- 2026-09-17: **Whole roster packed (`c4c9e78`) — still additive.** All 16 heroes: 2016 frames → **105 sheets**, 17MB → 12MB, every frame verified byte-identical. `--all` packs the roster and the registry is rebuilt by scanning every `sheets.json` on disk, so a single-hero run can no longer wipe the rest. Role guesses agreed with `heroes.json` across most of the roster; where they did not, the action is kept under its authored name and reported, never guessed (`Heavy_attack` on raven/void-raven reads as attack or skill; `BLOOD_MOON_IAIJUTSU`, `Kingdom_of_Cats`, `BONE_SPEAR`, `SOUL_DRAIN` match no role word). **Build #13** (`02e9551c-c47f-49d1-abda-9100d6328e30`, commit `491c8d2`) FINISHED and reached Apple — first build carrying the phone Skill bar + Sheet Lab. **Device-verified 2026-09-19 (emci, on-phone): Sheet Lab's side-by-side (per-frame art vs sheet crop) confirmed identical.** This is the gate the whole conversion plan was waiting on — passed. Review page (all 16 heroes, role picker): `https://claude.ai/artifact/3XrBTS6vj3o84eVJNCr4B7`.

- 2026-09-21: **The renderer itself now draws from sheets (`ecb304f`) — the Avatar, towers, and hero-bound bosses, not just the Sheet Lab preview.** `directionalClipArt` (skin.ts, the single frame-key choke point) got a sheet-aware sibling `directionalClipDrawable`: sheet first, legacy PNG fallback when a clip isn't packed. Since all 16 heroes are packed, every walk/idle/attack/skill/hurt/dash clip now resolves to a sheet crop — only the static 8-dir rotation fallback still reads per-frame art (untouched, out of scope). Two new draw components in `sheet-sprite.tsx`: `ClipSprite` (SVG board — towers, hero-bound bosses, nested `<Svg viewBox>`) and `ClipImage` (the Avatar overlay, which draws with `expo-image` OUTSIDE the SVG board sized by a Reanimated shared value — crops via percentage CSS instead of a pixel offset so it works with no JS-thread number). Reviewer hand-verified the crop maths against archangel's real sheet coordinates; all 2016 packed frames are square, matching the square-box assumption `ClipImage` makes. **Combat math/hitboxes untouched — display only.** **Build #14** (`58bb8cef-9c21-4bb1-b413-866c2b6ee5a8`) triggered with auto-submit, carries this change. **Not yet device-verified — this is the next gate:** check the Avatar, at least one placed tower, and (if reachable) a hero-bound boss on several heroes, especially the size extremes (`oni` 188px, `cyber-shinobi` 116px, `kitsune` 156px). **Still pending after this:** pack the remaining ~819 tile/icon/avatar assets (T-02), drop loose frames from the bundle once packed (T-03), Sprite HQ in-app (T-04), and the still-open Supabase decision (mapping stored in `heroes.json` vs a live-editable table) — deliberately held back from this build to keep schema changes isolated and reviewable on their own, per the standing rule on schema/auth/env changes.

- 2026-09-22: **OTA IS RESTORED — the whole sprite-sheet effort's actual goal, confirmed live.** Build #16 (`44ed5f37-4b49-4cb7-8adc-9734427a8ef9`) FINISHED and reached Apple; not yet device-verified. Three commits between #15 and #16, all pushed:
  1. `3030ee0` — dev row to bind a hero as a tower (the offer-sheet bind flow never fires for heroes `PLAY_EVERYTHING_FREE` auto-owns, so there was no way to reach a hero-bound tower on a fresh account).
  2. `8caee5a` — dev Heroes list reorganized to one row per hero (name + Own/Avatar/Bind chips) instead of the same 16 heroes listed three times over (48 rows) — emci called it "all over and clumpy" while testing.
  3. `f58b83b` — **the asset-count fix.** Bundle was 3401 exported assets (EAS Update's cap is 1000) even after the hero conversion, because (a) `kenney-td` (299 static tower/tile images) was never packed and (b) the hero animation/rotation loose PNGs were still bundled ALONGSIDE their sheets, doubling them for nothing. Fixed: `play-art-pack.ts --family` packs a flat same-size collection (kenney-td) into one grid sheet; `skinArtDrawable`/`roleArtDrawable`/`skinWalkDrawable`/`skinAnimDrawable` (sheet-aware siblings of the legacy skin.ts art functions) resolve `kenney-td/*` and hero rotation keys through sheets; `heroClipWalk`'s frame-counting reads the sheet registry instead of probing now-absent loose PNGs; `avatars`/`primal`/`tiles` (272 files) dropped from the bundle outright — `assets/play/licenses/README.md` already marked all three "no longer on the board", zero references anywhere in `src/`. **A first review of this commit FAILED**: only 4 of the ~10 render call sites that could read a packed family had been converted, leaving `fx.shot`, `unit.final`'s rotation fallback, and the creep/boss sprite block (walk/idle/static, including the Archangel-mapped `unit.final` band role) silently rendering blank or as a plain circle. Found and fixed every remaining call site in the same pass; a second review, checking every frame of every `skin.json` role rather than sampling, passed clean. Also caught and fixed in the same pass: `check-heroes.ts`/`check-creeps.ts` had gone silently DORMANT (every hero read as an unbundled stub, not a failure) once the loose keys they probed disappeared — both now also check the sheet registry.
  4. `8e96b99` — dev toggle for the board skin (Neon vs Grove Classic, `Board / Misc` section). Both paints were already fully bundled; `BOARD_SKIN` was just a hardcoded constant, so Grove Classic had never been reachable. Honest scope note left in the commit: towers and creeps have only ONE art pack each today, so — unlike the board — there is nothing to switch between for those yet; a real tower/creep skin switcher needs new art first, same as adding a new hero.

  **`npx expo export --platform ios` confirms 815 assets (was 3401).** `npm run ota:publish` — the actual test — **succeeded**: `816 iOS assets, 816 Android assets (maximum: 2000 total per update)`, published to `production` (update group `e98bf553-249f-484c-bf62-ca33c450280f`, commit `8e96b993`). This is the first OTA published since the Play merge blocked it. **Open for emci:** confirm build #16 on device (Sheet Lab across a few heroes, especially `oni`/`cyber-shinobi`/`kitsune`), then confirm the OTA that just published actually reaches that installed build (the board-skin toggle is the visible marker — Board / Misc → "Board skin: Neon Viper (tap to switch)" should be new). Icons/tiles (T-02/T-03) and Sprite HQ in-app (T-04) remain queued, no longer urgent now that OTA works again — every future JS-only change can go out in minutes.

- 2026-09-23: **FIXED (`a9c6424`) — packed sprites drew at the board's ORIGIN, not their pad.** Symptom: a bound hero (Sak, Void Raven) placed on a tapped pad rendered in the top-left corner; the Avatar rendered correctly. **Root cause:** `SheetSprite` drew each packed frame as a nested `<Svg x={x} y={y} width height viewBox>`. react-native-svg renders a nested `<Svg>` as a NATIVE VIEW laid out by React Native — `Svg.render()` (`node_modules/react-native-svg/src/elements/Svg.tsx`) reads `width`/`height` into a style and **never applies `x`/`y`** — so every sheet-cropped sprite drew at 0,0. The Avatar was unaffected because it draws through `ClipImage` (expo-image + CSS percentages inside a positioned `Animated.View`), which is exactly why it sat correctly at centre while bound heroes piled into the corner. **Fix:** a `<G transform="translate(x, y) scale(size/rect.w, size/rect.h)">` holding the sheet offset by `(-rect.x, -rect.y)` and clipped to the frame rect via `<Defs><ClipPath>` (per-instance `useId()`); same crop, correct position. Reviewer verified the transform matrix against react-native-svg's own parser, confirmed `G transform` / `clipPath="url(#id)"` / `Defs`-inside-`G` are all supported on 15.12.1, and that the 7 call sites + legacy branch are untouched.
  **Why nothing caught it, and what now does:** the dev Sheet Lab — the screen built to prove the crop, and the one emci device-verified as "identical" on 2026-09-19 — draws every sprite at **x=0, y=0**, the single position where "ignores x/y" and "honours x/y" render the same picture. typecheck, lint and all 85 offline checks passed on a board where no packed sprite could land on its pad. New `scripts/check-sheet-sprite.ts` (`check:sheet-sprite`, auto-discovered by the gate) closes that: it asserts placement at a **non-zero** position using a real packed frame with `rect.x > 0`, pins the **exact** transform string (translate AND scale values), asserts the sheet offset cancels the frame's position, that two frames crop differently, and structurally that a nested `<Svg>` never returns. Placement maths lives in `src/play/sheet-sprite-math.ts` so it executes in plain Node instead of only type-checking. **The first version of that check was toothless** — its "lands on its box" helper was an algebraic tautology and it only asserted `scale(` was *present*, so a wrong scale factor would have passed; reviewer caught it, it's now pinned to the literal transform and negative-tested (breaking the scale fails the check). Gate: 86 offline checks. **Shipped as an OTA** (update group `038c0b74-65b4-4aa0-a194-4a9b206bb7e9`) — "Uploading assets skipped, no new assets found", i.e. the restored OTA pipe working as intended. **Device-verified 2026-09-23 (emci): a bound hero now lands on the tapped pad.** Note for future device checks: expo-updates downloads an OTA in the background and applies it on the NEXT cold start — emci's first post-publish screenshot still showed the corner bug because the phone was on the previous JS; the You tab's `RunningUpdateLine` shows which update is actually running.
  **2026-09-24 — tower shots "not firing" was SIZE, not a render bug (`e93d556`, OTA `fafa39b8-ed3a-40b8-9b25-11f811ab980f`).** Measured, not guessed: the Kenney shot (`kenney-td/towerDefense_tile273`) is a 16px dot in a 64px square; at its 4.5-unit skin size on emci's iPhone board (870px across a 1320px 3x screenshot) the visible dot was ~3pt. emci confirmed on device it was there but tiny. New `SHOT_DRAW_SCALE = 2.5` (display only) → ~8pt dot. Lesson recorded: an earlier theory that the 4,094px-wide kenney-td atlas was too wide for the phone was stated without evidence and withdrawn — measure first. Evidence page: `https://claude.ai/artifact/8ef69Q3a8TRRfXcBCfC93Z`. Not yet device-verified at the new size.
  **UPDATE 2026-09-23 — the same fix ALSO cured the "invisible minions" bug (device-verified by emci: minions on the path with HP bars and damage numbers, bound heroes on their pads).** The prediction below that it would NOT was wrong. Best-supported mechanism (inferred, not proven): the board draws in a fixed layer order — towers → bound bosses → corpses → **puffs** → shots — and a nested native `<Svg>` view inside the board SVG disrupted every sibling drawn AFTER it, so once a bound boss (packed art → nested `<Svg>`) was on the board, the puffs and shots drawn later never appeared. That matches every observation: invisible only after placing a bound boss, and "dev-unlock only" merely because bound-boss placement was mostly done through dev rows. The disappearing round indicator sits OUTSIDE the board SVG and is not clearly explained by this — watch for a recurrence. Original (now superseded) note follows.
  **Still open, deliberately not chased in the same pass:** the "invisible minions" report. Ordinary creeps (`unit.puff`/`runner`/`tank`, and `unit.boss_scout`) resolve to `{kind:'legacy'}` and never touched the sheet path, so this fix is NOT expected to change them; only the Main-wave-10 boss (`unit.final` → Archangel) and `unit.tank_alt` use sheets. Two earlier investigations also established: `devUnlocked` is inert (one read, gates dev-kit visibility only — emci's "dev-unlock does more than flip a flag" hypothesis is refuted in code), `placeBoundBoss` is pure and preserves every wave/schedule field, `godMode` only suppresses loss-on-leak, and the Sentry hits `skinFootAt is not defined` / `not a function` are STALE — `skinFootAt` has been defined-but-uncalled since `9377f0b` (2026-09-15), nothing in the repo calls it, and the issue short-ids (ATO-APP-8/G) are among the first ever recorded. **Also still true and worth fixing separately:** the dev row "Place bound on next free pad" genuinely ignores the tapped pad by design (`boardMap.pads.findIndex(first free)` → pads[0] = `{x:25,y:25}`, the top-left pad), which is a second, independent source of the same-looking symptom.

- 2026-09-16: **Play — airport free loop: all 16 heroes owned + playable as Avatar and Bound Boss, with Batch 1+2+3 hero art ingested, on `play/grove-v0`.** **Art ingest:** unzipped `games/grove/ref/batch 2.zip` / `batch 3.zip` into `games/grove/ref/cast-source/heroes-batch2/` + `heroes-batch3/`, then copied every viable char into `assets/play/skins/cast/heroes/<slug>/` (rotations + E/W clip rows only, names normalized Corvus-style — pack `-deadbeef` hash stripped, `N._` ordering prefix stripped, spaces/case folded to the authored name). **11 new heroes:** `cyber-shinobi`, `elowen`, `kael` (Kael-09), `maldrath`, `morwen`, `neon-viper`, `raven`, `sak`, `frost-lich`, `velkhar`, `void-raven` — plus Batch 1's `aurex` + `kitsune`, whose art had never been copied (Aurex's idle row is asymmetric 13/12 E/W so it ships `idle: null`; Kitsune's attack shipped two hash-variant `west` rows, deduped to one). **Skipped:** INFERNA (empty) and `masterpiece_premium_dark_fantasy_side_scroller_pix`. **`heroes.json` now holds 16 rows** — new rows are `unlock: free_farm`, `acquire: "Free (airport)"`, `face: ew`, clips mapped honestly from folders (idle/walk/dash/attack/skill/hurt; `skill` = `Skill_*` or `Heavy_*`; `hurt` = `Hurt`/`Death`/`Soul_Stagger` where no plain hurt), and a unique `skillId` each. Aurex/Kitsune moved off "Coming soon" onto free. **Skills:** new `data/skills.json` + `skills-data.ts` — one thin veil variant per hero `skillId` (name + radius + damage + slow), wired so `castSlowPulse` (now takes a resolved `CastSkill`) soft-damages AND slows in radius (kills grant scrap), and `heroCastSkill(view.activeAvatarHeroId)` drives the Defend skill button copy + the cast numbers — the Avatar's skill now follows the hero it fights as instead of a hardcoded "Root Veil". **Free loop:** `PLAY_EVERYTHING_FREE` (mirrors `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV`) in `dev-mode.ts`; `loadPlayStore` runs `airportFree` to migrate `owned_hero_ids` = ALL hero ids on Play load (idempotent, no offer queued, no Premium gate). Bound Boss place already works for any owned hero via `isHeroBoundTower`/`resolveBoundHeroTowerKit`. **Path cast UNCHANGED:** Girl/Wizard/Knight + Oni/Archangel enemies untouched; the cycle boss map (Final→Archangel, Scout→Oni) is unchanged. `check-heroes` 24 → 29 → 29 (roster now 16, `AVATAR_READY_IDS` = all 16). Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, 55 pre-existing warnings) + `check:heroes`/`check:creeps`/`check:towers` green. **CastActor slot masks** (`cast-actor.ts` `CAST_ACTOR_CLIPS`) — avatar: idle/walk/dash/attack/skill/hurt, path (creeps/bosses on the road): idle/walk/death, tower (towers + Bound Bosses): idle/attack/skill — the art is shared, the slot set is not. **Premium parked** — everything free until the owner returns. - 2026-09-16: **Play — A6: Bound Boss place (a hero bound as a tower) on `play/grove-v0`.** A hero bound through the A2 own-sheet (`bindHeroAsTower` → `bound_bosses` record `stars ≥ 1`) now places on an empty pad like a tower, sharing the existing `BOUND_BOSS_MAX_ON_BOARD` (2) board cap and the A2 exclusivity + `BOUND_HERO_MAX` (2) list cap (both unchanged). **Documented stats pick (no per-hero RPG kit):** one fixed `HERO_TOWER_STATS` block for every hero tower — crystal-like chunk hits (22 atk / 1.3s cd / 20 range), auto-attack picks the highest-HP creep in range — and **FREE place for v1** (`placeCost 0`: the hero is already owned+bound, so no scrap while the hero-tower economy is undecided; GAME_SPEC §9k's `place_cost` is the cycle boss's fragment farm, not a hero bind). Auto-skill fires only when the hero authors `clips.skill` (Archangel `Ultimate_Final_Judgment`, Oni `BLOOD_MOON_IAIJUTSU`) on `HERO_TOWER_SKILL_COOLDOWN_MS` (12s, the archer tower default), damage = `baseAttack × TOWER_SKILL_DAMAGE_MULT` in `TOWER_SKILL_RADIUS` (same K1b pulse). Draw resolves `resolveBoundHeroTowerKit(heroId)` (the A6-prep path) through the SAME tower clip FSM (idle/attack/skill, sticky E/W, `towerAnimRef`/`towerFaceRef` keyed by the shared `nextId`). **Follow-up animation pass (same slice):** bound bosses seed their clip player on place (`clip: 'idle'`, `clipStartAt: now`, face `'e'`) so idle breathes from frame 0; the sim tick's shot/skill FX trigger `startTowerOnce('attack'|'skill')` on the hero's own kit; and a bound boss that resolves NO clips warns loudly in `__DEV__` instead of silently freezing on its static rotation. Bound bosses cap at 16 board units (tower-sized, not the hero's natural 24). **Cycle-boss ART mapping (PRODUCT LOCK):** the cycle boss's own def (`ember_sovereign`, `bound_bosses.json`) now also animates — `boundBossHeroId(bossId)` maps a cycle boss to its cast hero (`ember_sovereign → archangel`; a future Scout cycle boss → `oni`), so the Final cycle boss draws the Archangel kit (idle `Hover_Idle` / attack `Attack_01_Seraph_Strike` / skill `Ultimate_Final_Judgment`) through the SAME `resolveBoundHeroTowerKit` + tower FSM as a hero tower, while its LABEL and combat (`getBoundBossDef`, burst echo) stay untouched. `boundBossHeroId` resolves a hero-bound tower to its own id, so one helper drives all bound-boss art. Dev kit gains "Place bound on next free pad" under Heroes. `check:heroes` 24 → 29 (stats / cycle-art map / place / remove / hero-tower combat). Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, 55 pre-existing warnings) + `check:heroes`/`check:towers`/`check:creeps`/`check:kenney`/`check:wave-tables` green. **Not committed (emci asked to hold for the Expo Go pass) and NOT device-verified** — Expo checklist: Own Archangel → Bind → place on pad → idle breathes, shoots with attack anim, skill on CD; place Ember Sovereign (Final) → idle breathes + Seraph Strike on shot + Ultimate on skill CD; exclusivity with Avatar; cap 2; Oni/Scout the same if available.

- 2026-09-16: **Play — K2.5 + K3: the two path BOSSES breathe when stalled and fall when killed, from art already on disk, on `play/grove-v0`.** Supersedes the K2 entry's closing line ("bosses untouched … their idle/death is K3's call"). `assets/play/skins/cast/skin.json` gives `unit.boss_scout` (Crimson Oni — the scout mini-boss and the scout boss, `BAND_UNIT_ROLE`) and `unit.final` (Archangel Commander, the Main Final enemy) the same named `anims` slots the creeps use, on the same shared FSM: `unit.boss_scout` idle = `Breathing_Idle` (4f E/W), death = `EPIC_DEATH_FALL_OF_THE_CRIMSON_LEGEND` (9f E/W), `walk` stays `Walking`; `unit.final` idle = `Hover_Idle` (11f E/W), death = `Angelic_Flinch` (9f E/W), `walk` stays `Divine_Flight`. **Only ONE art copy was needed:** the Archangel rows were already bundled from the A3/H1.5 hero work under `skins/cast/heroes/archangel/animations/` (which is why the boss role points at the HERO folder rather than duplicating 128px frames), while the Oni's idle/fall rows existed only in `games/grove/ref/cast-source/heroes-batch1/Crimson Oni Animated Samurai Hero/animations/` — 26 PNGs (4+4 idle, 9+9 fall, E/W rows only, matching the role's 2-dir side-profile contract) copied into `assets/play/skins/cast/boss/oni/animations/` and `play-art-prep` re-run (registry **+26 keys exactly**, nothing else moved). No new code was needed in the renderer: `defend-screen.tsx`'s path-walker stance and the corpse path resolve role-generically through `puffUnitRole` → `isPathWalker` → `creepClip` / `creepClipFrame`, and both boss roles were already in `SKIN_PATH_WALKER_ROLES` — so the ONLY render change is one stale comment ("a role with no idle art (every boss, today)"), which is now false. **`scripts/check-creeps.ts` is extended from the three creeps to every path walker (5 roles, 8 → 10 checks)** rather than duplicated into a new file: the roster carries a per-role `artRoot` (`creeps/<id>` / `boss/oni` / `heroes/archangel`) instead of the hardcoded `creeps/` prefix, the alias table gains only the names that genuinely are a path slot (`Hover_Idle`→idle, `Divine_Flight`→walk, `Angelic_Flinch`→death, `EPIC_DEATH_FALL_OF_THE_CRIMSON_LEGEND`→death), and three new guards close the "no fake deaths" ask for real: (a) a **bidirectional** slot join — every DECLARED clip's folder must map back to that same slot, so wiring the Knight's `Iron_Slash` or the Girl's `Flower_Wave` into `anims.death` now FAILS instead of validating cleanly (proven with a negative test: pointing the Girl's `idle` at `Pick_Up_item` exits 1 with `actual: undefined, expected: 'idle'`); (b) `unit.puff` / `unit.tank` are pinned to declare **no** `death`, so a kill there stays the instant removal it has always been; (c) the roster must be a subset of `SKIN_PATH_WALKER_ROLES` in `src/play/skin.ts` (read as text — skin.ts imports the Metro registry and can't load under tsx), so a walker can't exist that the board would draw as an N/S-snapping rotation. - 2026-09-15: **Play — K2: creeps breathe when they stop, and the Wizard falls when it dies, on `play/grove-v0`.** The three path walkers now play their authored cast clips instead of holding a frozen walk frame: `unit.puff` (Village Girl) / `unit.runner` (Wizard) / `unit.tank` (Knight) in `assets/play/skins/cast/skin.json` gain named `anims` clips on the SAME cast clip-kit schema the heroes and towers use — `idle` for all three (`Breathing_Idle`, 4f E/W) and `death` for the Wizard alone (`death`, 9f E/W), because the Girl's and the Knight's packs ship no death art and their kill still reads as the instant removal it was. **No art was copied and `play-art-prep` was not re-run:** all 260 creep PNGs and their registry keys were already tracked from H1.5 — the folders were in the bundle and nothing read them. The decision table is shared, not buried in the screen: `src/play/cast-kits.ts` gains `CREEP_PATH_CLIPS` (death/walk/idle), `CREEP_STILL_MS` (250ms, the ONE stall threshold, now shared with the walk tick), and the pure `creepClip()` / `creepClipFrame()` — death > walk > idle, a slot the art does not author is skipped rather than invented, and a death one-shot HOLDS its last frame so a corpse never stands back up (the idle loops). `src/play/skin.ts`'s `SkinAnimClipName` gains `death`; the Avatar's own clip map narrows it back out (`Exclude<…, 'death'>`) because the Avatar never dies — a leak plays `hurt` plus the lost overlay. Render (`defend-screen.tsx`): the stance signal is the walk tick's OWN measured rate (one threshold, no second clock) — no path progress for `CREEP_STILL_MS` ⇒ the breathing loop on a wall-clock cadence seeded per creep so a stalled cluster does not breathe in lockstep; still advancing ⇒ the walk cycle exactly as before, **including a vine-slowed creep, which keeps its (slower) leg cycle rather than breathing**; a role with no idle art (every boss, today) keeps holding its last walk frame exactly as it did. The engine has no corpse state, so the board keeps its own: the same vanished-puff diff the damage floaters already use turns a kill into a short-lived corpse carrying the pose it was last drawn in (role / box / lane / sticky E/W face), plays the death clip once and drops the body at `CREEP_CORPSE_MS` (1.1s ≈ the 0.63s one-shot plus a held beat); every run reset (start / retry / abandon / board switch / replay preview) clears them. **Creep ATTACK stays parked** — the Wizard's `attack`, the Knight's `Iron_Slash` and the Girl's `Flower_Wave` / `Pick_Up_item` are deliberately unreferenced (no barracks, no path attack, no air-slash until K3). New `scripts/check-creeps.ts` (`npm run check:creeps`, auto-picked-up by `check:ota-gate`'s package.json scan) runs 8 checks: roster + rotations resolve in the art registry, each declared clip's `frames` = registry = PNGs on disk on both E/W rows, the ladder's priority/fallback/hold rules, the data⇄ladder join per role, and 3 source assertions that the board actually calls the ladder. Its one new ENFORCE rule is the K2 ask — a creep whose pack SHIPS an idle/death/walk folder must declare it — proven with a negative test (dropping the Girl's `idle` fails with the pointed *"the pack ships Breathing_Idle … but the role declares no idle"* message); folders belonging to no path slot are REPORTED as parked, never failed. Two things found on the way: the Knight's west row carries the pack hash on the FACING segment (`Walking-feaba143/west-0247c925`) while the registry strips every segment, so the disk↔registry comparison matches facings by their hash-stripped name (a literal `west` lookup counts 0 files and would have reported a truncated bundle that is not one) — `check-heroes` / `check-towers` share that latent assumption and were left untouched as out of scope; and the pack spells the locomotion slot three ways (`Walk` / `walk` / `Walking`), so the check matches a folder named after its slot whatever its casing, plus a two-entry alias table (`Breathing_Idle`, `Walking`) for the genuinely different names. Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, 55 pre-existing warnings) + `check:creeps` 8/8 + `check:heroes` 24/24 + `check:towers` 8/8 green. **A reviewer pass over the finished diff caught two real bugs, both fixed before this report** (and both worth knowing about, because each was invisible in the code that read correctly): (1) the corpse clock lived only in the walk tick, which is gated on `phase === 'running'` — but the kill that ENDS a wave ends the run on the SAME tick it creates the corpse, so the last kill of every run froze on frame 0 of its death clip and its body sat on the board for the whole results screen, never pruned; the fix is a second, narrow interval that ages corpses out (and thereby advances their wall-clock frames) only while the wave clock is stopped (won / lost / paused) and a body exists — while the wave runs, the walk tick still owns it, so there is no second render clock. (2) The stance read the walk tick's `rate` flag, but a freshly seeded creep has no measured rate yet — so every creep BREATHED for its first ~50–150ms before walking; the stall is now read from how long ago that creep last advanced (`Date.now() - entry.at > CREEP_STILL_MS`), the same threshold, seeded so "not measured yet" means moving. Both are `defend-screen.tsx`-only; the ladder's semantics changed in no way. The review also closed a hole in `check-creeps.ts`: its "honest stub" branch `continue`d before comparing frames, so a declared clip whose PNGs were on disk with a STALE registry (art-prep not re-run) was reported as a harmless stub and the check still passed — it now fails unless the folder is genuinely absent. Reviewed-and-accepted nit, not fixed: the three source-text regexes on `defend-screen.tsx` are satisfiable by comments, so they are a wiring smoke test, not proof. **NOT committed (emci asked to hold until the Expo Go pass) and NOT device-verified** — the device checklist is swarm/runner/tank walk, breathe idle when stopped, the Wizard's death pop on kill, and no air-slash. Bosses (Crimson Oni / Archangel enemies) are untouched: they walk, and a stalled boss still holds its walk frame — their idle/death is K3's call.
