- 2026-09-10: **Core loop redesign — standing policy change: clean-slate, not staged migration, for every remaining card.** Recorded in full in `docs/CORE_LOOP_REDESIGN_PLAN.md`'s new top-of-doc "STANDING POLICY" section — read there first. Short version: this app is pre-launch with no real user data, so the earlier caution (build new alongside old, verify, only drop old in a later separately-approved step) was protecting against a risk that doesn't exist, and it already cost real work — reroll got built against `category_question_items` and the old Legends matcher, and a whole unplanned legend-candidates content tool got built for a library the new system never uses, all scheduled for deletion anyway. Going forward: delete old tables/RPCs/components as part of the same pass as the new system, don't investigate reuse/migration/coexistence, and when something old (e.g. `/roll`'s legend item) depends on what's being deleted, stub it out to degrade gracefully rather than rewire it (separate, unrequested scope) or try to preserve the dependency. Schema changes still need emci's explicit approval and a look at the exact migration first — only the staging is what changed, not the approval gate. Applies immediately to the in-progress Legends rewrite (old T-12/T-13) and anything discovered later that still touches an old system this plan replaces.

- 2026-09-09: **Core loop redesign — follow-up fix: `addToBankPool`'s row-matching hardened from prompt-text equality to array-position matching.** T-03's review flagged (but deliberately deferred, out of that card's scope) a latent fragility in T-02's `src/lib/questions/bank-pool.ts`: `addToBankPool` matched `insert_bank_pool_items`' returned rows back to input drafts via a `Map<prompt, id>` lookup keyed on prompt text — fragile because the RPC applies its own `left(trim(...), 400)` server-side, and any future divergence between that and the client's own string would silently leave `bankItemId` unset (surfacing later as a thrown error in `store.ts`, not where the real problem was). Investigated whether this is a live bug today: it isn't — `parseQuestionDraft` already trims and caps every AI-generated draft at 400 chars before it ever reaches this function, so the server transform is currently a no-op — but the design shouldn't have needed to lean on that invariant to be correct. **Fixed to match by array position instead**, extracted into a new pure function `assignBankItemIdsByPosition` in a new file `src/lib/questions/bank-pool-match.ts` — split out (not just a new function inside `bank-pool.ts`) because `bank-pool.ts` imports `@/lib/supabase`, which transitively pulls in react-native and can't be loaded by a plain Node/tsx script; same reasoning `src/lib/rolls/results.ts` was already split from `rolls/store.ts` for. Verified directly (not assumed) that `insert_bank_pool_items` (wave50) genuinely guarantees output-row-order-matches-input-order: single sequential `for ... jsonb_array_elements` loop, one `return query insert ... returning ...` per iteration, no ORDER BY anywhere, and `on conflict do update ... returning` (not `do nothing`) always yields exactly one row per input item — so row count always equals draft count, and any mid-loop failure rolls the whole call back (the client sees `error`, never a partial array). New `scripts/bank-pool-check.ts` (`check:bank-pool`, 4 assertions, auto-picked-up by `check:ota-gate`) covers positional assignment, the RPC's real duplicate-prompt-collapses-to-one-shared-id behavior, a row-count-mismatch throw, and an empty-input no-op. **Review round found 2 real issues in the check script's own first draft, fixed before ship:** a "duplicate prompt" test asserted 3 distinct ids where the RPC's actual `on conflict do update` behavior returns the same id twice (test's expectation was simply wrong, not the code) — corrected to assert the real shared-id result and reframed what the test actually demonstrates; a second test claiming to simulate "server-side value transform" was vacuous given the row type only carries `id` — removed rather than padded. No callers (`ongoing-round.ts`, `store.ts`) needed to change — they already treated `addToBankPool` as opaque. Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, 55 baseline warnings unchanged) + new `check:bank-pool` (4/4) + full `check:ota-gate` (71/71) all green, run before and after the review-fix pass. No schema/auth/dependency changes. Pushed to `master`.

- 2026-09-09: **Core loop redesign — T-03 shipped: ongoing-round save path + UI trigger, closing out T-01/T-02's dormant infra.** New `saveOngoingRoundBatch`/`fetchLatestOngoingRoundPack` (`src/lib/questions/store.ts`) persist one composed 25-item round via `insert_ongoing_round_pack` (wave50, still not applied to the live DB) in a single RPC call — one call per round, not per chunk, since that RPC creates exactly one new `question_packs` row each time it's called. New `src/lib/questions/run-ongoing-round.ts` (`runOngoingRound`) wires `composeOngoingRound`'s real dependencies (`generateOngoingRoundBatch`, `bank-pool.ts`, `fetch-recent-texts.ts`) with a deliberately no-op `saveItems` — the real save happens once, after the full round is composed, not from inside the incremental-save hook `composeOngoingRound` calls per bank draw/AI chunk. New `OngoingRoundFold` (`src/components/questions-fold.tsx`), rendered once `fullProfileLocked` (the frozen 50-question intake is fully answered): starts a round via `runOngoingRound` or serves/answers the next unanswered item through the same `answerQuestionItem` + `updateTraits` + `earnTokensQuiet` path Infinite Questions' own `pick()` already uses. **One critical bug found and fixed via review before push, not by emci or a later session:** `fetchLatestQuestionPack` (Infinite Questions' `loadLatestPack`) queried `question_packs` with no `kind` filter — wave49 gives `kind` a `NOT NULL DEFAULT 'infinite_questions'`, and this same diff is what makes `kind='ongoing_round'` rows exist for the first time, so the moment one exists it would sort ahead as "the latest pack" and Infinite Questions would start serving/answering/skip-resting a 25-item ongoing round instead of its own daily pack. Fixed by scoping both readers to their own `kind` (`fetchLatestQuestionPack` → `infinite_questions`, `fetchLatestOngoingRoundPack` → `ongoing_round`) — confirmed via grep that these are the only two `question_packs` readers in the codebase. Also fixed in the same review pass: `saveOngoingRoundBatch` now refuses an empty drafts array with a clear client-side error instead of letting a 0-item payload hit the RPC's own Postgres exception; two `check:ongoing-round-save` assertions that were trivially satisfied by an import line alone are now anchored to the actual deps object passed to `composeOngoingRound`. **Known, accepted gap, documented not fixed (out of this card's scope, flagged for a future session via `spawn_task`):** `insert_bank_pool_items` (wave50) truncates prompts to 400 chars server-side, but `addToBankPool` (`bank-pool.ts`, T-02) matches the RPC's returned rows back to drafts by the untruncated prompt string — a >400-char AI-generated prompt would leave `bankItemId` unset and now (since this card's `saveOngoingRoundBatch` guard) discard the whole round, not just that item. Low real-world likelihood, not touched here since it's T-02's code. Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, 55 baseline warnings unchanged) + new `check:ongoing-round-save` (10/10) + full `check:ota-gate` (70/70) all green, run through two review rounds. No schema/auth/dependency changes — calls RPCs from wave49/wave50, both already reviewed/pushed as schema-only migrations, still not yet applied to the live DB. Pushed to `master` (`cd80a3b`).

- 2026-09-09: **Core loop redesign — T-02 shipped: `fetchRecentTexts` + bank-pool bank-first/AI-fallback wired into `ongoing-round.ts`, plus the schema prep it needed.** `supabase/migrations/wave50_ongoing_round_prep.sql` (schema-only, NOT yet applied — needs emci's review/apply): widens `question_items.sort_index`'s check constraint from `< 5` to `< 30` (a 25-item ongoing round needs headroom past Infinite Questions' 5-item cap); adds `insert_ongoing_round_pack` as a NEW sibling RPC for 25-item `kind='ongoing_round'` packs (every item requires a non-null `question_bank_item_id`) — `insert_question_pack` itself is untouched, and this new RPC isn't called by any code yet (T-03's save path will call it); adds `bump_bank_times_served`/`insert_bank_pool_items`, the only client write path onto `question_bank_pool` (wave49 revokes insert/update/delete from authenticated by design) — both cap their input array at 25 items, added in review (a shared, cross-user table shouldn't accept an unbounded array from one caller). New `src/lib/questions/fetch-recent-texts.ts` (`fetchRecentTexts()`, matching `category-batch-store.ts`'s proven `fetchAskedQuestionTexts` pattern, plus the full static `QUESTIONS_BANK` — always fully "answered" by the time an ongoing round exists) and `src/lib/questions/bank-pool.ts` (`fetchBankCandidates`/`recordBankUsage`/`addToBankPool`, the client wrapper around the new RPCs). `ongoing-round.ts`'s `composeOngoingRound` now bank-drafts from `question_bank_pool` first (per axis, least-served, excluding this user's permanent `question_bank_reroll_exclusions`, skipping near-duplicates against `recentText`) and only sends the shortfall to `fillAxisCountsChunked`'s existing AI path — every AI draft gets written into the bank pool (`addToBankPool`) before being included, so every returned draft (bank-drawn or fresh) carries a `bankItemId` (`QuestionDraft` gained this new optional field). Kept the module Supabase-import-free — the three bank functions are new required deps on `ComposeOngoingRoundDeps`, injected the same way `generateBatch`/`saveItems` already are. New `ONGOING_ROUND_META` call-site metadata (`src/lib/ai/call-sites.ts`) + `generateOngoingRoundBatch()` (`generate.ts`) — not yet called by any production code (no caller wired up until T-03). **One real, critical bug found and fixed via this session's own review before push, not by emci or a later session:** an earlier draft of wave50 re-added a `question_items_axis_check` constraint (16 axes, incl. `playfulness`), believing wave17's original 15-axis check was still live and missing `playfulness`. It is not — `wave21_playfulness_categories.sql` already replaced it with `question_items_axis_known` (16 axes), and `wave27_drop_stale_question_items_axis_check.sql` exists specifically to drop the old name so the two can't coexist. wave49's own comment ("re-verified — confirmed stale, no fix needed") was correct all along; this session's own plan-time claim to the contrary (and a stale note in `docs/CORE_LOOP_REDESIGN_PLAN.md`'s build-order section, now corrected in place) was wrong. Fixed by dropping that section from wave50 entirely before push — verified directly by reading wave21/27's actual SQL, not re-trusting the earlier claim. **Other review findings, fixed:** the two new RPCs' missing item-count caps (above); two new `scripts/ongoing-round-check.ts` test cases were missing (partial bank coverage, a non-fatal `recordBankUsage` failure) — added, and in writing them found a second real bug, this time in the TEST file itself: two mocks' fake-`generateBatch` prompt-naming reset an index to 0 on every call, so an axis whose count spans two chunks (e.g. extraversion needing 3, split 2+1 across chunk boundaries) generated textually-identical prompt text twice — the real, correct `isNearDuplicate` filter then rejected the second occurrence as a genuine duplicate, silently costing the round 1-2 items in a way that looked like "the AI just came up short." Fixed by using a monotonic counter across calls, matching the pattern the file's first test block already used correctly (`generated.size`). Also added `scripts/wave50-check.ts` (5/5, schema-only source assertions, mirrors wave45-49's pattern) — a gap the first review round flagged: every other wave migration in this repo has one and runs in the gate, wave50 didn't. Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, 55 baseline warnings unchanged) + `check:ongoing-round` (5/5, rewritten) + new `check:wave50` (5/5) + `check:ai` (bumped `AI_CALL_SITES.length` 8→9 for the new real call site) + full `check:ota-gate` (69/69) all green, run through two full review-fix rounds (first found the critical axis-check bug + the two warnings above; second confirmed all fixes and found nothing new). Verified: `check:legends64` still 12/12 (all 384 combinations still compose, no double-article/run-on regressions, jargon-guard clean), typecheck clean, lint 0 errors/55 baseline warnings unchanged.
