- 2026-09-06: **profile_100 moved off profile_percent onto a new bank_percent metric, resolving the open question from the entry directly below.** Emci's call: keep `profile_50` on `profile_percent` (settledCount-based) as its own mid-point milestone, but give `profile_100` a metric that can actually reach 100 from finishing the bank — `bank_percent = (bankTotalProgress(tracks).answered / bankTotalProgress(tracks).total) * 100`, computed in the same `crossedMilestonesFor` helper alongside the other two metrics (now 3: `bankTotalProgress`, `profile_percent`, `bank_percent`). `checkMilestones` again needed zero changes. **Reviewer's one real finding:** `profile_100` (bank_percent) and `answers_48` (bankTotalProgress) now always cross together, since both reduce to "every bank question answered" from the exact same `bankTotalProgress(tracks)` call — two back-to-back toasts every time someone finishes the bank ("You have answered every question in the bank." then "You've completed your full profile!"). Not fixed — flagged as a copy/product duplication for Emci to weigh in on later; the existing toast queue (built for T-06) already handles the double-crossing correctly, no clobbering. A stale comment claiming only `profile_percent` could co-cross with `bankTotalProgress` was corrected to name the actual guaranteed pair. `check:milestones` grew from 17 to 19 assertions (reviewer mutation-tested the new ones against three mutants — removed call, wrong formula, wrong metric string — all caught). Confirmed via fresh grep: `isProfileSettled`/`milestones_celebrated`/badge-chip system untouched, `profile_50`'s def byte-identical. Gate: typecheck + lint + all 51 offline checks green, run before and after the comment fix. Pushed to `master` (`f175883`). **New open item:** whether `answers_48` and `profile_100` should be merged, re-worded, or one dropped, since they'll always fire as a pair.

- 2026-09-05: **profile_50/profile_100 added to MILESTONE_DEFS and wired into the live update path — first milestone feature to touch intake-sweep.tsx's core logic since the per-axis defs (still just config).** New `profile_percent` metric = `(settledCount(tracks) / TRAIT_AXES.length) * 100`, computed inside a new module-level `crossedMilestonesFor(tracks, celebrated)` helper in `intake-sweep.tsx` that now runs at BOTH pre-existing call sites (the T-04 backfill effect and T-05's `refreshAfterAnswer`), returning the merged crossed-defs list across `bankTotalProgress` and `profile_percent` together — `checkMilestones` itself needed zero changes, confirmed by review (empty diff on that function). **Flagged to Emci, not silently fixed:** reviewer found `profile_100` is effectively unreachable from bank answers alone — `settledCount` sums each axis's `effectiveStability` (a decayed EWMA capped near 1.0 only after ~10 *perfectly consistent* answers per axis) and rounds; the 48-question bank gives only 3 answers/axis, so the bank-alone ceiling is ~56% of profile_percent, well under the 100 threshold. `profile_50` is reachable but only for an unusually consistent full-bank answerer. Left as-is because `settledCount` is the same "settled" value already surfaced elsewhere as "N of 16 settled" (`trait-stability.ts`) — swapping to a literal per-axis boolean count (e.g. counting axes above a stability threshold) would read the profile differently from that existing label, so this is a product call, not a bug fix, and is called out here for Emci to weigh in on rather than changed unilaterally. Also fixed at review time: a stale comment claiming two milestones could never cross in the same pass (now false — bankTotalProgress and profile_percent can both cross on one answer; the existing toast queue already handles it correctly, just the comment was wrong), an inaccurate JSDoc line, and a brittle verbatim-string check-script assertion swapped for a scoped regex anchored to the new helper's body specifically. `check:milestones` grew from 15 to 17 assertions. **Confirmed via fresh grep:** `isProfileSettled`, `milestones_celebrated`, `badges.ts`, `check-milestone-badge.tsx` all untouched — only referenced in comments. Gate: typecheck + lint + all 51 offline checks green, run before and after the review fixes. Pushed to `master` (`3e4fba3`). **Open question for Emci:** keep `settledCount` (matches the existing "N of 16" label, `profile_100` stays a long-run/high-consistency milestone rather than a bank-completion one) or switch to a stricter per-axis boolean count so `profile_100` is reachable by finishing the bank alone — no code change made pending that call.

- 2026-09-05: **Per-axis-completion entries added to MILESTONE_DEFS — config only, not wired into any caller yet.** Investigated first (read-only) which of four candidate metrics were real, trackable values today: streak/consecutive-day check-ins turned out to be **not real** (only an all-time `checkCount` bucketed into a `presenceTier`, no day-streak logic anywhere — `use-growth.ts`/`growth.ts`); overall profile completion % turned out **not real** either (`settledCount`/`bankTotalProgress` exist but neither is reduced to one 0-100 number anywhere); "legends unlocked" turned out to be **not a metric at all** (only a whole-tab lock via `isProfileSettled`, no per-legend unlocked count); per-axis completion was the one that's **already real and read**, via `effectiveStability(row, now) > 0` and `axisVariant(tracks, axis)`. Emci picked per-axis completion to build on that finding. `MilestoneMetric` widened from the single literal `'bankTotalProgress'` to `'bankTotalProgress' | \`axisComplete:${TraitAxis}\`` (template literal type), and a new `AXIS_COMPLETE_DEFS` — one entry per `TRAIT_AXES` axis — was spread into `MILESTONE_DEFS`: id `axis_complete_<axis>`, metric `axisComplete:<axis>`, **threshold read from the real bank size via `bankQuestionCount([axis])`, not hardcoded to 3**, so it stays correct if the bank's per-axis draft count ever changes. `checkMilestones` itself needed zero changes — its `def.metric === metric` filter was already generic enough to support a per-axis metric namespace, exactly per Emci's "reuse as-is" constraint. **Reviewer caught a real copy bug:** the plain humanized axis names for `growth_mindset`, `locus_of_control`, and `self_efficacy` are exact matches in this repo's `framework-fence.ts` PHRASES ban-list (a runtime guard against generated copy naming a psychology framework by name) — fixed with a small `AXIS_DISPLAY_OVERRIDES` map (growth/control/confidence) for just those three axes, plus a new fence assertion in `check:milestones` so this can't silently regress if more axes are ever added. Reviewer's other warning (a self-referential test — `assert.equal(threshold, bankQuestionCount(...))` would still pass even if an axis had a 0-question bank, making that milestone unconditionally pre-crossed) fixed with an added `threshold > 0` assertion; not live today, every axis has exactly 3 bank drafts. `check:milestones` grew from 12 to 15 assertions. **Confirmed via fresh grep/review:** no import cycle (`questions/local.ts` doesn't know `milestones.ts` exists), no id/metric collision with the 4 existing `answers_*` entries or `badges.ts`'s `BADGE_IDS`, `isProfileSettled`/`milestones_celebrated`/the badge-chip system all untouched. Gate: typecheck + lint + all 51 offline checks green, run before and after the fence fix. Pushed to `master` (`a9e62de`). **Deliberately not done, out of scope for this task:** none of these 16 new defs are wired into `intake-sweep.tsx` or any other caller yet — they exist in `MILESTONE_DEFS` only. Wiring them (deciding what triggers the per-axis check — presumably the same `refreshAfterAnswer` path, passing `axisVariant(tracks, axis)` as `currentValue` for whichever axis was just answered) is a separate future task.

- 2026-09-05: **T-06: generic MilestoneToast built and wired into the T-05 crossing check — the milestone feature chain (T-01 through T-06) is now feature-complete end to end.** New `src/components/milestone-toast.tsx` exports `MilestoneToast({ title, body, reduceMotion, onDone })`, forked from `FullProfileUnlockAck`'s fade shape (`check-milestone-badge.tsx:332-362` — same `withSequence(withTiming(1,{duration:900}), withTiming(0,{duration:1200}))` non-reduced timing and hard `setTimeout(2400)` reduceMotion cut) but generic: title/body are props, not hardcoded copy, so any future milestone type reuses this one component. Added an `onDone` callback (absent in the fork source, which just fades and stays mounted forever) fired via a matched-duration `setTimeout`, with cleanup on both branches — reviewer confirmed it fires exactly once per mount and is Strict-Mode/early-unmount safe. `src/app/(tabs)/intake-sweep.tsx`'s `onMilestoneCrossed` (previously a module-level `console.log` stub) is now a component-scoped callback that enqueues crossed defs into a `toastQueue` state array rather than showing them directly — a defensive-but-currently-unreachable guard against two crossings landing in the same `refreshAfterAnswer` pass (not possible today: `bankTotalProgress` moves by at most 1 per answer and thresholds are 12 apart, but the queue keeps that true even if a future metric changes it). `reduceMotion` now sourced from `useAppearance()`, same hook `check-milestone-badge.tsx` already uses. **Caught mid-build, before review:** the first render of `<MilestoneToast>` had no `key` prop — since `activeToast` changes identity but the JSX position doesn't, React would have reused the same component instance across queued toasts, and the second toast's fade-in effect (deps `[reduceMotion, opacity]`, neither of which changes between toasts) would never have replayed. Fixed by keying on `activeToast.id`; `check:milestones` grew a dedicated assertion pinning the `key` so this can't silently regress. Reviewer's three remaining warnings were fixed at review time (two check-script assertions were weak — a near-vacuous `.includes('title:')` check and a hardcoded-copy guard that only covered milestone titles, not bodies — both tightened to check against the real `MILESTONE_DEFS` content and the prop type signature) and one accepted as-is (a future caller passing an unstable `onDone` would read a stale first-render closure inside the component's effect; safe today since the only caller, `dismissActiveToast`, is a zero-dependency `useCallback`, so not fixed to avoid overengineering an unreached case). `check:milestones` grew from 10 to 13 assertions. **Confirmed via fresh grep:** zero touches to `isProfileSettled`, `milestones_celebrated`/`markMilestoneCelebrated`, `badges.ts`, or `check-milestone-badge.tsx`'s `MilestoneBadges`/`CheckMilestoneBadge`/`BADGE_IDS` (that persistent Home chip strip is a fully separate mechanism from this transient toast; `FullProfileUnlockAck` in that same file was read as a fade-pattern reference only, never edited). Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, only pre-existing warnings) + all 51 offline checks green, run before and after the key fix. Pushed to `master` (`237641e`). **Nothing left queued on this feature** — the next real decision is product, not engineering: whether/when to add more `MILESTONE_DEFS` entries or metrics beyond `bankTotalProgress`.

- 2026-09-05: **T-04/T-05: milestone crossing detection wired into the bank-progress update path — still no toast UI (T-06 next).** Builds directly on the same-day scaffolding entry below. `src/lib/me.ts` gained `persistCelebratedMilestones(userId, ids)` (read-modify-write, `Set`-deduped merge into `celebrated_milestone_ids`, mirrors `markMilestoneCelebrated`'s shape but for the array column — kept fully separate, that function is untouched). All wiring lives in `src/app/(tabs)/intake-sweep.tsx`, the one screen where `me` and `tracks` are both already loaded and where every bank-answer write (`QuestionsFold`/`IntakeSweep`) already funnels through `onUpdated={refreshAfterAnswer}`. **(1) Backfill (T-04):** a new mount-time effect, ref-guarded to run its body once, computes `checkMilestones('bankTotalProgress', bankTotalProgress(tracks).answered, me.celebrated_milestone_ids ?? [])` and silently persists any already-crossed defs — no toast call. A new `backfillReady` state gates both Questions UI blocks alongside the existing `tracksReady`/`flagsReady` gates, closing the race where a fast first answer could fire the real check before backfill lands. **(2) Post-answer check (T-05):** `loadTracks` now returns the freshly fetched tracks (was void) so `refreshAfterAnswer` isn't reading one-render-stale React state; it recomputes `checkMilestones` against the by-then-backfilled `celebrated_milestone_ids`, persists any newly-crossed defs, and calls a module-level `onMilestoneCrossed(def)` stub (`// TODO(T-06): show a toast/celebration UI`) — currently just a `console.log`. **Reviewer found one real hole, fixed before push:** if the backfill's persist call failed, the effect still marked itself done and let the Questions UI open — the very next real answer would then treat those already-crossed-but-unpersisted defs as "new" and wrongly fire the placeholder for old milestones, exactly the bombardment T-04 exists to prevent. Fixed by resetting the one-shot ref on failure so a later mount/dependency change retries the backfill instead of assuming success. Reviewer's two remaining warnings were accepted as-is, not fixed: the Questions UI now blocks on one extra write round-trip with no loading fallback for a hang (narrow, same risk shape as the pre-existing `tracksReady` gate), and `persistCelebratedMilestones`'s read step discards its error the same way `markMilestoneCelebrated` already does (single-device, non-atomic, pre-existing pattern). Also strengthened at review time: `check:milestones` grew from 6 to 10 assertions, including a positive check that `refreshAfterAnswer` really does call `onMilestoneCrossed` (so deleting that call site fails the suite, not just silently passes) and a named-failure message on the source-anchor assertions. **Confirmed via fresh grep, both passes:** zero touches to `isProfileSettled`/its ~10-12 call sites, zero touches to `milestones_celebrated`/`markMilestoneCelebrated`/`use-growth.ts`/`nav-pixel.tsx` — that presence-streak system stays fully separate. No toast UI exists yet anywhere. Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, only pre-existing warnings) + all 51 offline checks green, run twice (once before the reviewer fix, once after). Pushed to `master` (`67e68b9`). **Next step, not started (T-06):** replace the `onMilestoneCrossed` console.log stub with real toast/celebration UI.

- 2026-09-05: **Milestone scaffolding added — `celebrated_milestone_ids`, `MILESTONE_DEFS`, `checkMilestones` — groundwork only, not wired anywhere.** Requested as three isolated task cards, explicitly not to touch `isProfileSettled`/its call sites and not to wire into `bankTotalProgress` yet. New `celebrated_milestone_ids?: string[]` on `Me` (`src/lib/me.ts`, optional — `Me` rows come from unchecked `data as Me` casts, so a required field would lie until the migration is applied everywhere; first real reader must default to `[]`), backed by `supabase/migrations/wave43_celebrated_milestone_ids.sql` (`add column if not exists ... default '{}'`, idempotent per this repo's existing wave*.sql convention). New `src/lib/milestones.ts`: `MILESTONE_DEFS` (4 entries, ids `answers_12/24/36/48`, metric `'bankTotalProgress'`, thresholds 12/24/36/48) follows the flat-typed-array style of `CATEGORY_DEFS` in `categories.ts`, not `badges.ts`'s per-predicate style. `checkMilestones(metric, currentValue, celebratedIds)` is pure, does not mutate its inputs, returns defs at/above threshold not already in `celebratedIds`. **Deliberately kept separate from the existing, already-live `me.milestones_celebrated`/`markMilestoneCelebrated` presence-streak mechanism** (read/written every mount via `NavPixel` → `useGrowth`, confirmed live not dead during investigation) — different shape (`string[]` vs `Record<string,string>`), different id space, no shared code, flagged to emci before building to avoid the two being confused later. `bankTotalProgress` itself already existed (`src/lib/questions/local.ts:192`, returns `{answered, total}`, total=48 = 16 axes × 3 bank drafts) — reused, not reinvented; `checkMilestones`'s `currentValue` for that metric is meant to be `bankTotalProgress(tracks).answered` computed by the (future) caller. New `check:milestones` (6 assertions), auto-picked-up by `check:ota-gate`'s `check:*` discovery. Reviewer's first pass flagged two real issues fixed before push: migration wasn't idempotent (`add column` → `add column if not exists`, matching every other `me` column-add migration) and ids used hyphens (`answers-12`) instead of the requested underscore form (`answers_12`) — fixed in both `milestones.ts` and its check script. Gate: typecheck + lint (0 errors, only pre-existing warnings) + all 51 offline checks green. Migration file created but **not applied** to the live DB per emci's choice (review it yourself via Supabase SQL editor/CLI). Pushed to `master` (`bf91a3c`). **Next step, not started:** wire `checkMilestones('bankTotalProgress', bankTotalProgress(tracks).answered, me.celebrated_milestone_ids ?? [])` into an actual caller/screen, decide where the celebration UI lives, and decide when `celebrated_milestone_ids` gets persisted back (mirroring how `markMilestoneCelebrated` does it for the other system) — none of that exists yet.

- 2026-09-04: **Inconsistent-answerer floor on `effectiveStability`, three staleness fixes (Explore/Sage/capstone badge), and an additive category picker on the Questions screen — requested together, red-teamed before building, implemented in order with sign-off at each design fork.** (1) **The floor.** An axis where every answer disagrees with the running EWMA by >= 0.5 holds `stability` at exactly 0 forever (0 is a fixed point of the recurrence at zero agreement) — before this, no amount of re-answering cleared it, silently blocking `isProfileSettled` for that user forever. New `STABILITY_FLOOR_OVERRIDE_N = 8` / `STABILITY_INCONSISTENT_FLOOR = 0.05` in `trait-stability.ts`: once `answerCount` reaches the override, `effectiveStability` returns `max(decayed, floor)` instead of raw (possibly-zero) stability. Read-side only — stored `stability` and `applyEwmaAnswer` untouched, so decay math and future EWMA updates operate on the real numbers. Values sanity-checked with emci via concrete before/after scenarios before implementing (per emci's explicit request), confirmed via `AskUserQuestion`. New `scripts/trait-stability-check.ts` (`check:trait-stability`) proves the trap is real and the floor works, using a constructed *adversarial* answerer (`signal = current.value <= 0.5 ? 1 : 0`) rather than a fixed alternating pattern — a fixed pattern like 0.9/0.1 turned out NOT to stay trapped (the EWMA value converges toward the middle, delta shrinks below 0.5, and it would clear the gate on its own); only the adaptive worst case, where `max(v, 1-v) >= 0.5` for every `v`, guarantees agreement stays exactly 0 by construction. (2) **Staleness sweep found a real bug independent of the ask.** Investigated every `isProfileSettled` call site for null/undefined/race issues (requested read-only, wait-for-ok). Answering a question calls `updateTraits` → `persistMe`, whose write patch never sets `updated_at` (no DB trigger does either) — so Explore, Sage Title, and Sage insight (keyed on `me?.updated_at`) and the capstone milestone badge (keyed on `[userId]` only, mount-once) never refreshed mid-session after an answer landed; a user could settle their last axis and still see "locked" until the screen unmounted. Fixed per emci's approved option: key those effects on the `me` object reference instead (mirrors Home's `[userId, me, timeZone]`, which already worked because `MeContext.refresh()` returns a new object every call). `MilestoneBadges` gained an optional `me` prop (dependency-trigger only, not read in the body); its one live call site (`you.tsx`) passes it. Capstone fix landed as a same-pattern follow-up request after the first three shipped. (3) **Category tagging on the 48 bank questions**, deterministic rule confirmed with emci: first `CATEGORY_DEFS` entry (in defined order) whose `axes` include the question's axis. Added as an optional `QuestionDraft.category` field (required would have broken `parse.ts`'s AI-generated drafts, which have no fixed category — caught before it broke typecheck). Caught a second latent bug while wiring it through: `local.ts`'s `copyDraft` rebuilt every defensive copy from three named fields, silently dropping `category` on every call — fixed, or the picker would never have seen it. (4) **Category picker — design corrected mid-build, re-confirmed with emci before continuing.** Original plan (filter persisted items by stored category) turned out to be a dead end: the Questions screen renders one `QuestionItemRow` at a time from `question_packs`/`question_items`, and `saveQuestionPack` only ever writes `axis`/`prompt`/`options` to that table — `category` never reaches the DB, and adding it there would be a schema change (not done, no sign-off sought, per the standing rule). Rebuilt instead on the existing `priorityAxes` mechanism (the same lever `focusAxis` deep-links already use in production): picking a category resolves its axes via `getCategoryDefs()` and prepends them, deduped, ahead of the existing deferred/focus priority list, through a new pure `mergeCategoryPriority()` helper (`questions/deferral.ts`) so it is independently unit-tested rather than inline/untestable component logic. Empty selection is a no-op by construction — the picker cannot alter the existing axis-driven rotation, sweep, or skip machinery when unused, satisfying emci's isolation requirement. Fails open in the UI too: a broken/empty category catalog just hides the picker row rather than blocking the question below. Inherits one pre-existing limitation from `focusAxis`, not introduced here: if today's pack already has an open item for a different axis, `routeQuestions` serves that cached item regardless of priority. **Extra defensive coverage for the no-test-window OTA**, per emci's ask: `check:trait-stability` (new script, 6 assertions) plus category/merge/wiring assertions folded into `check:questions` (27 assertions total) — including a source-text assertion that the picker wires through the shared helper (not a duplicated inline version) and never references `category_id` (confirming it stayed off the schema-change path). Gate: typecheck + lint + all 50 offline checks green, run twice (once after the first four changes, once after the capstone-badge follow-up). Every diff shown to emci before commit, per instruction — nothing committed mid-session.

- 2026-09-04: **The variant selector is wired end-to-end — the static bank alone can now settle an axis.** Closes the "flagged, not done" item from the entry directly below, which shipped 48 drafts and `bankDraftFor(axis, variant)` but left every caller on variant 0, so drafts 2 and 3 were reachable API-only and nobody ever saw them. New `axisVariant(tracks, axis)` returns that axis's **report-track `answerCount`**, and `bankDraftFor` wraps on it: 0 answers -> draft 1, 1 -> draft 2, 2 -> draft 3, 3 -> back to draft 1. Three answers on an axis are now three DIFFERENT questions, which is exactly the `answerCount >= 3` that `effectiveStability` requires — so `isProfileSettled` is reachable from static content, with no model call and no quota spend. `composeLocalSweep(tracks)` and `composeLocalQuestionBatch(recentAxes, priorityAxes, tracks)` replaced their short-lived numeric `variant` param with real tracks (that knob was one commit old and only ever used by a check, so nothing external broke). Both call sites feed it: `guardedBatch` passes `input.tracks`, `routeQuestionSweep` passes `input.tracks` from a new `tracks` prop on `IntakeSweep`. **Four decisions worth remembering.** (1) **Gut-call must never advance the question** — `axisVariant` reads the `report` track only, matching the rule that `self_game` never counts toward settled; letting it move the draft would desync what a person is shown from what actually counts. Asserted directly. (2) **`onUpdated` for the sweep now refreshes TRACKS, not just `me`** — this is load-bearing and was nearly missed: the Questions tab passed `refresh` (me only), so after answering, `answerCount` in local state never moved and the person would have been re-served the identical question. Switched to `refreshAfterAnswer`, with a source assertion pinning it so a future edit cannot silently revert it. (3) **The sweep effect keys on a `variantKey` string, not on `tracks`** — `tracks` is a fresh array on every fetch, so putting it in the deps would refetch the sweep continuously; the joined per-axis count vector changes only when a count actually moves. (4) **Rotation and selection are now cleanly separated** — `preferFreshAxes` decides WHICH axes lead a batch, `push` decides WHICH draft; both used to flow through the draft object, which is what made the duplicate-slot bug possible in the first place. **Known limit, deliberate:** `IntakeSweep` filters through `unansweredSweep`, which drops any axis that already holds a trait value, so the sweep still fills each axis once and effectively never shows variants 2 and 3 — the rotating pool above it is what delivers answers 2 and 3. Tracks are passed to both surfaces anyway so they stay consistent (and to cover an axis whose value was cleared but whose track survived). Making the sweep a genuine repeat pass means changing that filter; not done, not asked. Coverage in `check:wave19`: the three-then-wrap walk, per-axis isolation (advancing openness leaves the other 15 byte-identical), gut-call exclusion, an exact-count fixture that self-asserts its own `answerCount` (the first draft of it was off by one — `applyEwmaAnswer(null, ...)` is itself the first answer, so seeding outside the loop double-counts), guard-cleanliness across all 48 drafts rather than just the 16 leads, and source assertions that both call sites really pass tracks. Gate: typecheck + lint + all 49 offline checks green.

- 2026-09-04: **Question bank expanded to 3 per axis (48), `bankByAxis` stops dropping content, skip-the-rest stays inside Questions, and the full-picture capstone now reads `isProfileSettled` with a one-time unlock celebration.** Direct follow-on from the same day's research pass, which found the supply problem behind the new gate: 16 static questions for 16 axes meant the static bank alone could never settle a single axis (`effectiveStability` needs `answerCount >= 3`). **(1) Bank is 48 drafts**, 3 per axis, grouped in `TRAIT_AXES` order with each axis's original locked draft first — `QUESTIONS_FEW_SHOTS` quotes several verbatim and `check:questions` asserts three of them, so a group must not be reordered or a first entry reworded without updating that string. Multiple-choice / true-false only, 2-3 options (the `insert_question_pack` RPC and `parseQuestionDraft` both reject anything else; 4 options WOULD be a schema change). Writing these is more constrained than it looks: the framework fence rejects `secure`, `anxious`, `avoidant`, `competitive`, `accommodating`, `collaborative`, `compromising`, `neurotic` outright, so the natural phrasings for the attachment and conflict axes all had to be written around. Every stem and option is asserted guard-clean (fence -> jargon -> phrase) and prompt-unique. **(2) `bankByAxis` now returns `Map<TraitAxis, QuestionDraft[]>`** instead of first-wins `Map<TraitAxis, QuestionDraft>` — it had been silently discarding every draft after the first, which is why a second bank question per axis would have been dead content. New `bankDraftFor(axis, variant)` (wraps modulo, returns a defensive copy so a caller mutating options cannot edit the bank) and `bankLeadDrafts()`. `composeLocalSweep`/`composeLocalQuestionBatch` take an optional `variant` defaulting to 0 = prior behaviour exactly. Also fixed a latent bug the regrouping would have caused: `composeLocalQuestionBatch` used to hand the whole bank to `preferFreshAxes`, which dedupes by axis — with 3 drafts per axis that would have spent batch slots on duplicates it then drops, returning fewer than 5 items. It now dedupes to one draft per axis first. **(3) "Skip the rest" no longer `router.replace('/')`** — the sweep already lives ON the Questions tab, so the old code ejected the person to Home, the one place nothing asks again; it now refreshes `me` and scrolls back to `QuestionsFold` at the top of the same screen, which has just inherited the skipped axes as `priorityAxes` via `me.question_deferred`. Skipping must defer, never escape the gate. **(4) The `full-picture` capstone reads `isProfileSettled(tracks)`, not `allCategoriesReady(tracks)`** — a bar category counts as ready on 2 of its 3 axes, so the badge could light up while axes sat unsettled and the AI surfaces stayed locked; badge and locks now agree on one predicate. Copy moved from "All 11 categories have settled" to axes. New `src/lib/full-profile-unlock.ts` holds the persisted seen-flag; `FullProfileUnlockAck` reuses Explore's `NotedAck` `withSequence` fade with a `reduceMotion` branch, the strip auto-opens once so the moment is not hidden behind a collapsed fold, and the flag is burned BEFORE the celebration renders so a re-render or second mount cannot replay it (asserted by index comparison in `check:badges`). **Flagged at the time, DONE the same day (see the entry above):** the 32 new drafts shipped reachable through `bankDraftFor` but not yet served, because nothing passed `variant > 0`. `axisVariant` now selects the draft from each axis's report-track `answerCount`, so the static bank alone can settle an axis. **Decision emci should revisit:** `hasSeenFullProfileUnlock` lives in AsyncStorage, not on `me`, because a `me` column is a schema change needing sign-off — the cost is that it is per-device, so a new device replays the celebration once. **One assertion changed value, not behaviour:** `questions-check` pinned `rotated[0].axis === 'attachment_avoidance'`, which depended on the bank's old interleaved order; grouping by axis makes it `conscientiousness`. The rotation rule is untouched — the literal was updated and a property assertion added (none of the three recent axes appear at all) so the test checks the rule rather than an ordering artifact. Coverage: `check:badges` 14 -> 19, plus new bank/variant assertions in `check:questions` and `check:wave19`. Gate: typecheck + lint + all 49 offline checks green.

- 2026-09-04: **Every AI-spend surface now gates on one predicate — `isProfileSettled` (all 16 axes past the stability floor) — replacing the weaker/absent checks the audit found.** New `isProfileSettled(rows, now)` in `trait-stability.ts` is true only when EVERY axis's report track clears `effectiveStability > 0` (answerCount >= 3 AND enough agreement to survive decay). Strictly stronger than both existing predicates: `isProfileComplete` needs one answer per axis with no agreement requirement, and `!isThinProfile` reads a stability SUM over 6/15 of the inventory, so a few deep axes could clear it while others sat at zero. Two behaviours, deliberately split: **surfaces that lock** (Explore observations, Sage Title, Sage insight) render `PROFILE_LOCKED_COPY` ("Complete your profile to unlock this") plus an "Answer Questions" link to `/intake-sweep?axis=<missingAxis>`; **surfaces that stay silent** (daily card, Sage chat) swap to the deterministic `localProvider` — real content every day, no lock screen, no quota claim, no tokens. `routeExplore` gained a `locked` ExploreKind that returns before `loadLatestPack`, so a decayed profile stops serving yesterday's pack rather than showing stale observations. **Two findings that changed the shape of the work:** (1) the bank could NOT be the daily card's static fallback — `content.generated.ts` only holds Days 1-3, so `bankCardForMe` returns `null` on day 4+ and would have rendered a blank card; the local provider composes for any day and is already the no-key fallback, and there is now an assertion pinning that. (2) `explore-panel.tsx` never passed `tracks` to `routeExplore` at all, so its completeness gate was dead code from the day it landed — now wired (the component still has no importer in app code; only check scripts read its source). **Deviation from the brief, flagged:** Sage insight is a paid surface (spends notes), so the Spend button locks rather than the model call returning a static line — never charge notes for a canned string. **Crisis verified untouched:** `detectCrisis` runs at `talk.ts:116` and returns at 123-131, before the quota claim and before `buildProviders`; the gate sits after it. Pinned by three assertions — a behavioural one (flagged message on empty tracks → `kind: 'crisis'`, 0 model calls, 0 claims, flag still logged), a source-order one in `floor-check.ts`, and the Explore equivalent. Infinite Questions and The Story left untouched — they already gate correctly. **Convention that needs watching:** `tracks: undefined` means "caller supplied no track state" (labs, ~20 existing check call sites) and leaves the gate OFF; `tracks: []` is a real "nothing settled" and gates. Documented at all four gates, but a future surface that forgets to pass `tracks` is silently ungated. **Two risks emci should decide on separately:** (a) an axis where each new answer lands >= 0.5 from its EWMA holds agreement at 0 forever, so an inconsistent answerer can never unlock any surface — there is no floor or override; (b) conversely `decayedStability` approaches 0 asymptotically and never reaches it, so decay never actually re-locks a settled user. Coverage: 7 new assertions in `check:voice` (52 total), 4 new in `check:explore` (35 total), `check:floor` strengthened. Gate: typecheck + lint + all 49 offline checks green.

- 2026-09-04: **Fixed the two credit-leak gaps from the read-only audit — Explore observations and Sage Title now check profile completeness before spending a paid AI call, matching Questions/Story.** Confirmed via investigator the gap was still live (no fix had landed since the audit). `src/lib/explore/route.ts`: `useLocal` now also goes `true` when `isThinProfile(settledCount(input.tracks ?? [], input.now))` — forces local compose, no `claimAiCall`, mirroring `questions/route.ts:169`'s "thin profile must never spend a paid call" rule; uses the caller's `input.now` (not a fresh `Date.now()`) so decay evaluation stays consistent with the rest of the routing logic. `src/components/sage-title-card.tsx`: same check added to the branch just before `claimTitleGenerate()`, coexisting with the pre-existing (much weaker — only needs 2 stable axes) `titleReady()` early return. **Flagged for emci, not changed:** users with roughly 2–6 settled axes will now see the "Not enough settled yet" empty state on Sage Title instead of an AI-generated one (previously they'd get a generated title on as few as 2 stable axes) — an intentional side effect of closing the gap, worth knowing about. **Known tradeoff, out of scope:** `dev-lab.tsx`'s Explore-regen dev tool doesn't fetch `tracks`, so it now always takes the local path when testing — real production callers (`explore.tsx`) already pass `tracks` and are unaffected. `scripts/explore-check.ts` gained a `verySettled()` fixture helper (8 EWMA reps, ~0.95 stability/axis — 3 reps only reaches ~0.58, not enough for the new summed-stability check) and a dedicated test proving a thin profile skips the claim; 3 pre-existing remote-path tests needed a non-thin `tracks` fixture added to keep exercising the guard/model-call path now that the gate exists. **Notable hiccup:** the `explore/route.ts` half of this fix was silently lost between editing and the first commit — verified/tested correctly beforehand, but the commit only picked up the other two files, and the file reverted to its pre-fix content with no diff to stage. Re-applied and re-verified (`check:explore` 32/32, typecheck clean) before a second commit; cause not identified, worth watching for recurrence. Reviewer: PASS on the original 3-file diff. Gate: typecheck + lint clean, `check:explore`/`check:questions`/`check:sage-load` all green, full `check:ota-gate` green (49 checks, run by reviewer). Pushed to `master` (`7c3cfa1`, `497e5fa`). **A second, separate hiccup:** a local-only commit (`e86ffda`, "Revert 'fix: gate Explore observations...'") appeared reverting `sage-title-card.tsx`/`explore-check.ts` back out — not made by this session, never pushed. Surfaced to emci directly rather than silently pushing or discarding it; confirmed unintentional, undone with `git revert e86ffda` (commit `98f612e`), re-verified (`check:explore` 32/32, typecheck clean), pushed. Two unexplained local reverts of this same fix in one session (this one, plus the earlier route.ts edit that vanished before its first commit) — worth watching for a pattern (concurrent session/tool touching this repo?) if it happens again.

- 2026-09-04: **Reverted: the separate "Back" button on the intake screen (entry below) was removed — collapsed back to one button.** Follow-up on the entry directly below. Investigator re-confirmed `onBackToLogin` was byte-for-byte identical to `handleSignOut` (both just `clearLocalSession()`) and that no non-destructive "show login while still authenticated" path exists in this architecture (`_layout.tsx` gates `auth` strictly on `!isAuthed`) without new scope (a login re-entry UI calling `signInWithPassword` directly, never proposed as needed). Asked emci: two buttons doing the identical thing was judged not worth keeping, since nothing is lost by signing out at this phase (no `me` row exists yet to preserve). - 2026-09-04: **Separate "Back" button added to the first intake screen ("Introduce yourself"/`AccountStep`), alongside the existing Cancel/sign-out link.** Requested by emci. Investigation surfaced a real constraint before building: the root navigator (`src/app/_layout.tsx:72-78`) only mounts the `auth` (login) screen when `!isAuthed`, and only mounts `onboarding` when `isAuthed && !hasMe` — by the time a user sees "Introduce yourself" they already have a live Supabase session (created in the `auth` screen just before), so there is no route that shows login while still signed in. Asked emci directly: confirmed the new `onBackToLogin` handler should do the same thing the existing Cancel/sign-out does (clear the session via `clearLocalSession()`) so the guard naturally flips to the login screen — just as a separately-named handler/button for future divergence, not a distinct non-destructive path (none exists today without changing the auth-gating architecture, out of scope). Implementation: `onBackToLogin` defined next to `handleSignOut` (`onboarding.tsx:347`), same signingOut/busy guard; new `‹ Back` Pressable added inside `AccountStep` only (new `backButton` style, mirrors `core-intake-sweep.tsx`'s existing back-button pattern); `AccountStep` gained `onBackToLogin`/`signingOut` props, wired only on the `phase === 'account'` render branch. Reviewer's one finding (Back button wasn't disabling/relabeling during the in-flight sign-out like the existing link does) fixed before push. Existing "Wrong account? Sign out" link/handler and other phases' `onBack` handlers (intake→account, optional→optional-gate) confirmed byte-for-byte untouched. Gate: typecheck + lint clean (only pre-existing unrelated warnings elsewhere). Pushed to `master` (`e957e79`). - 2026-09-04: **QA override invite code re-homed to a new `app_secrets` table (wave42) — wave41's `ALTER DATABASE` approach doesn't work on Supabase's managed hosting.** Emci tried the `ALTER DATABASE postgres SET app.qa_override_invite_code = '...'` command from the entry below in the Supabase SQL editor; it failed with `42501: permission denied to set parameter` — the dashboard SQL editor role isn't the database owner/superuser on hosted Supabase, so `ALTER DATABASE ... SET` is blocked outright, not a permissions thing either of us could grant around. First fallback considered was storing it as a column on the existing `app_config` table (same one `signup_mode` lives on) — **caught before implementing**: `app_config` has a public-read RLS policy (`app_config_select_all`, `qual: true`), so literally any client, signed in or not, can already `select` every column on it; a secret column there would have been fetchable straight from the app, worse than the original ask. Corrected to a brand-new `public.app_secrets` table instead — RLS enabled, **zero policies**, and `anon`/`authenticated` explicitly have no grants on it at all, confirmed live (`pg_policies` count 0, only `postgres`/`service_role` show up in `role_table_grants`). This mirrors a pattern already in this schema (`account_deletions`, `dev_access_grants`: RLS on, no policies, reachable only from inside `SECURITY DEFINER` functions running as the table owner, which is exempt from RLS since `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` isn't set). `assert_invite_usable`/`complete_signup` were re-pointed at `app_secrets.qa_override_invite_code` (single row, `id = 1`) instead of `current_setting(...)`; `check:invite`'s 7 live anon-probe checks re-run clean against the new functions. **The secret value still isn't set** — next step is emci running `update public.app_secrets set qa_override_invite_code = '...' where id = 1;` in the SQL editor (a plain `UPDATE` works fine there, unlike `ALTER DATABASE`). `docs/GOTCHAS.md` updated to describe `app_secrets`, not the dead GUC approach. Pushed to `master` (`d92ba77`). Not yet OTA'd (no client code changed in this entry, DB-only).

- 2026-09-04: **Registration flow: sign-out on every phase, invite code validated before advancing, QA override invite code added (schema change).** Three fixes, one investigated-and-closed. (1) The "Wrong account? Sign out" link only rendered on the first of 4 onboarding phases (`onboarding.tsx`) — a user stuck partway had no way out but killing the app; moved above the phase switch so it shows on all 4. (2) Invite code was only checked at final `complete_signup`, screens after entry — `continueFromAccount()` now calls the existing peek-only `assertInviteUsable()` RPC (same one `auth/index.tsx` already used) before advancing, same inline-error `Field` pattern as the handle check. (3) **New `wave41_qa_override_invite_code.sql` migration, applied live** — a QA-only invite code that always validates and is never consumed/expired, read from a Postgres database setting (`app.qa_override_invite_code`) rather than an `invite_codes` row, so the actual value never enters git or the app bundle. Both `assert_invite_usable` and `complete_signup` were rewritten (`CREATE OR REPLACE`) to check it first; confirmed via reviewer the non-override path is byte-identical to the prior live logic, and `check:invite`'s 7 live checks still pass. **The actual secret value has not been set yet** — emci is setting it manually via the Supabase SQL editor (`ALTER DATABASE postgres SET app.qa_override_invite_code = '...'`) rather than having this session run it, since that command was blocked by the sandbox's auto-mode classifier as a sensitive DB-wide action. Noted in `docs/GOTCHAS.md`: the setting only applies to new connections (pooled ones already open won't see it right away), it's `anon`-probeable so needs a high-entropy value, and override signups leave `referred_by = null` with no `invite_codes` audit trail. **Investigated, not a bug:** the reported "handle shows reserved when it should be free" — live DB check confirmed the `me_handle_unique_lower` index is already case-insensitive, the `me_handle_check` CHECK constraint's 7-word blocklist matches the client's `RESERVED_HANDLES` list exactly, and there are zero orphaned `me` rows. No fix applied; needs an exact repro (the specific handle + exact message text) to investigate further if it recurs. Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks + `check:invite` (live). Reviewer: PASS. Pushed to `master` (`eb0f01b`). Not yet OTA'd.

- 2026-09-04: **Every "N of 16"/"N of 9" completion count in the app is now directly actionable — tapping it lands you in that axis's questions.** Investigator audited every location first (Explore header, Profile Fill fold rows, Full Profile fold — already actionable, Categories fold, the locked "full picture" milestone badge, Legends — already actionable, the Questions tab). Design choice, explicitly confirmed with emci over the larger alternative: everywhere without one obvious axis already in hand navigates via the existing, already-shipped `/intake-sweep?axis=<axis>` deep link (the pattern Legends already used) rather than building new inline "Answer this one" editors at every site — smaller diff, reuses proven code. Six changes: (1) all 16 Profile Fill fold rows (`profile-fill-fold.tsx`) are now tappable; (2) Legends' local `missingAxis()` helper extracted into `trait-stability.ts` so other screens can share it, behavior-preserving; (3) the Explore header's "N of 16 settled" text is now tappable, jumping to the least-settled axis; (4) Categories fold (`categories-fold.tsx`) now lists **not-ready categories too** (previously invisible — only ready ones showed at all), each tappable via a new `missingAxisForCategory()` helper in `categories.ts` that stays scoped to that category's own axes; (5) the locked "full picture" badge (`check-milestone-badge.tsx`) is now tappable, jumping to the system-wide least-ready axis; (6) the Questions tab's collapsed fold title now shows a live "N of 16 unanswered" count via a new `unansweredAxisLabel()` helper, so it's no longer a mystery whether there's anything to do before opening it. **Two review findings fixed before push:** Categories fold's title still said "N of M **showing**" after the render change made every category visible, not just ready ones — misleading, reworded to "N of M **ready**"; the newly-tappable Explore header had no `accessibilityLabel` (VoiceOver would have read only the raw count with no hint it's actionable) — added "…Tap to answer more." **Flagged, not changed:** every not-ready category's **name** (including "Levity," called out in this repo's CLAUDE.md as diagnosis-adjacent, copy-review-gated content) is now shown to all users from day one — previously an unready category was fully invisible. Only the bare name renders, no generated copy/body, and the `CATEGORY_COPY_REVIEWED`/`CONCEPT_COPY_REVIEWED` gates are untouched, but this is a real visibility change worth emci's confirmation. Also noted but not unified: three different "which axis needs an answer" implementations now exist (`missingAxis` in trait-stability.ts using `me`'s raw column values, `missingAxisForCategory` and the milestone badge's local `leastReadyAxis` both using tracks-only `answerCount`) — can pick different axes for the same user in edge cases; cosmetic, not a bug, left as three small purpose-built functions rather than one over-general one. Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks, both before and after the review fixes. Reviewer: PASS. Pushed to `master` (`09ae715`). Not yet OTA'd.

No code-side setting exists: no `supabase/config.toml` in this repo, no migration or Edge Function configures GoTrue token TTLs, so nothing here controls it. No fix applied; nothing to verify in this repo.

- 2026-09-03: **Fixed: Observations "It landed"/"Not this" taps looked like they saved even when the write failed.** Emci reported the reaction buttons on Observations (Explore tab) didn't visibly update, plus asked about the "how you're currently leaning" 0-of-16 count and the legend/badge unlock. Root cause of the tap bug: `react()` in the live screen `src/app/(tabs)/explore.tsx` (and its unused duplicate `src/components/explore-panel.tsx`) fired the "Noted." acknowledgment optimistically *before* `await recordExploreReaction(...)` resolved, and any RPC failure was swallowed by a `console.log`-only catch — so a failed save still flashed "Noted." with no lasting change and no error shown. Fixed: ack now fires only after the write succeeds; failures clear any stale ack and show a small "Didn't save. Try again." line (new `EXPLORE_REACTION_ERROR` in `src/lib/explore/copy.ts`). **First review pass caught that the fix had been applied only to the dead-code duplicate (`explore-panel.tsx`, never imported/rendered) — refixed on the actual live component in `explore.tsx`.** The other two reported items were investigated and are **not bugs**: the "0 of 16" count is *settled* count (needs 3 agreeing answers per axis, `STABILITY_FLOOR_N` in `trait-stability.ts`), already labeled "settled" not "answered," and a direct UI path to answer axes already exists (the "Answer this one" button on every row in `FullProfileFold`) — no new UI needed. The legend/`full-picture` badge (`check-milestone-badge.tsx` + `categories.ts`) correctly gates on every category reaching that same settled threshold; logic checks out, so if a profile still isn't showing it, the profile genuinely hasn't hit 3-per-category yet (not independently verified against live DB state this session). Gate green: typecheck + lint (only a pre-existing, untouched `react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` warning) + full `check:ota-gate` (49 checks). Reviewer: FAIL → fix moved to live file → PASS. Pushed to `master` (`9ebd358`). Not yet OTA'd.

- 2026-09-03: **ATO+ (Zen/Neon/Anime appearance modes) temporarily unlocked for all users via `ATO_PLUS_FOR_ALL = true` in `src/lib/subscription.ts`.** Requested by emci; there is no real billing wired anywhere in this repo (`useSubscription()` was already a hardcoded-inactive stub, confirmed by investigation), so this only changes what the appearance picker shows as locked — no payment/entitlement system was touched, and no user data or DB row is written by the flag. Fully reversible: flip the const back to `false` (or delete the override block) and republish; nothing else to clean up, since appearance choice lives only in AsyncStorage and `resolveAllowedAppearance` already falls a re-locked viewer back to the default mode instead of stranding them on a mode they can't pick. **Known UX tradeoff, not fixed here (flagged by reviewer, out of scope):** a user who picks Neon while this is on will silently lose it back to Soft on next launch when the flag is turned back off, with no explanation shown — worth a heads-up message before re-locking if this runs for more than a short window. Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks. Not yet OTA'd.

- 2026-09-03: **Fixed: unexpected sign-outs — Supabase auto-refresh now ticks on app foreground/background (`src/lib/supabase.ts`).** Emci reported the app kept signing them out. First hypothesis (`changeAuthPassword()`'s `signInWithPassword` re-verify tearing down the session on a wrong current password) was investigated, proposed, and then disproven by reading `node_modules/@supabase/auth-js` directly — that call returns on error without touching session state; reverted before commit, no net change to `auth-password.ts`. Real cause: RN throttles JS timers while backgrounded, so the client's internal refresh tick can miss its window; by the time the app foregrounds, the access token has often fully expired rather than just being near-expiry, and GoTrue's `_callRefreshToken` treats an expired-token refresh failure as "genuinely dead" and hard-signs-out (`_removeSession()`) instead of the soft-preserve path it takes for a still-valid token. Fix: an `AppState` listener calls `supabase.auth.startAutoRefresh()`/`stopAutoRefresh()` on active/background — the exact pattern documented in the `@supabase/auth-js` JSDoc itself, web excluded (web manages its own `visibilitychange` refresh internally; calling `startAutoRefresh()` there would tear that down). Reviewer passed with two non-blocking notes: this reduces the failure window rather than eliminating it entirely (a long-enough background stretch can still hit the same path), and the module-scope listener is fine for an app-lifetime singleton client (no cleanup needed) but will register twice under dev Fast Refresh (harmless — old client's ticker self-stops). Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks. Not yet OTA'd — worth a real device background/foreground test before publishing.

- 2026-09-03: **"Build" row on You/Dev Tools Hub gains a publish date/time line, from `Updates.createdAt`.** Investigation first found the actual ask ("OTA info in the AI-provider panel") was already built and pointed at the wrong screen — `ai-lab.tsx` is a separate 5-tap-away provider screen, not where OTA info lives; the existing `RunningUpdateLine` (`src/components/running-update-line.tsx`) already showed group/channel/runtime with tap-to-copy. Also caught mid-build: the new row wrapper needed a plain RN `View`, not `ThemedView` without a `type` prop, which would have painted an opaque background fill the original row never had. `check:running-update` 5→7 checks, deterministic via a shape-regex (not an exact string, since the format is intentionally device-local and would be timezone-flaky in CI). - 2026-09-03: **`PROFILE_FILL_COPY_REVIEWED` flipped to `true` — the first `*_COPY_REVIEWED` flag ever flipped in this codebase (eight others still `false`).** Emci's exact instruction: "Confirm PROFILE_FILL_COPY_REVIEWED copy is ready for review, then flip it to true if it reads correctly," leaving "A faster pass" (`routeQuestionSweep`) deliberately ungated, confirmed correct as-is. Read the four Full profile checklist strings — a plain filled/unfilled data-state description ("N of 16 filled", axis names, "Complete", "Every trait has at least one answer") with no trait interpretation or character claim, not in Story/Levity's diagnosis-adjacent lane — and judged them safe to flip on that delegation. **Reviewer correction applied:** my first-pass reasoning cited passing the `containsFrameworkTerm` fence as grounds for flipping; the reviewer correctly pointed out that fence is not the flip bar — every other clean copy module (`storyCopyClean`, `conceptCopyClean`, etc.) passes it and still ships `false`, because the fence only rules out banned type language, not tone or fitness to ship. Reworded `docs/GOTCHAS.md` to cite emci's flag-specific delegation as the actual reason, and pinned the flag with `assert.equal(PROFILE_FILL_COPY_REVIEWED, true)` in `traits-check.ts` so it can't silently drift back to `false`. **Also fixed in the same pass:** the copy/fence-check originally lived inside `profile-fill-fold.tsx` (a React Native component), which crashed `tsx` when wired into a check script (esbuild can't transform `react-native/index.js`'s Flow syntax pulled in transitively) — extracted to a new pure `src/lib/profile-fill.ts` (mirrors `full-profile.ts`), matching every sibling copy module's pattern; the component's inline `'Not answered yet'` was deduplicated to reuse the existing `NOT_ANSWERED_YET` from `full-profile.ts`. `check:traits` 32→33, `check:copy-review-badges` still 9/9 (structural — asserts the badge JSX exists and is `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV`-gated regardless of the flag's value). Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks. **The draft-copy badge on this fold no longer renders for anyone**, dev or not — copy is live as final.

**Four strings emci can spot-check in 30 seconds** (`src/lib/profile-fill.ts`): "Full profile" (label) · "One answer is enough to fill a trait in. Filling one is not the same as settling it — settling takes a few answers that agree." (lede) · "Complete" (complete-state label) · "Every trait has at least one answer. Questions can go anywhere from here." (complete-state lede).

- 2026-09-03: **"Full profile" checklist on Explore + AI question generation gated on profile completeness.** New `filled` predicate (`answer_count >= 1` on the **report** track) added alongside the existing `settled` (`>= 3` + agreement): `isAxisFilled`/`filledAxes`/`unfilledAxes`/`filledCount`/`isProfileComplete`/`filledAxisLabel` in `trait-stability.ts`. **No migration** — same `answer_count` column, new derived predicates only. New `profile-fill-fold.tsx` on Explore (between TraitBandsFold and FullProfileFold) lists all 16 axes ticked/unticked with "N of 16 filled", switching to a "Complete" title at 16/16; it reads the tracks Explore already fetches. **Gate:** `routeQuestions` now runs consent → crisis → cached pack → **completeness** → quota → generate; an incomplete profile is served from the static bank with its unfilled axes first and **claims no quota** (a thin profile can no longer spend a paid call). Tracks reach the route by new plumbing: `intake-sweep.tsx` fetches them and passes through `QuestionsFold` → `RouteQuestionsInput.tracks`; absent tracks read as incomplete (safe direction). `routeQuestionSweep` ("A faster pass") is deliberately **not** gated — its job is to fill every axis in one pass, so gating it would make it unreachable exactly when it is most useful; noted in its doc comment, flagged to emci rather than decided unilaterally. Sage chat (`routeTalkReply`) is a separate path, never gated — verified. **Three review criticals/traps caught and fixed before push:** (1) the tracks refetch was first keyed on `me.updated_at`, which **never changes on a trait write** (`traitPatch` writes only trait values / `trait_sources` / `trait_touched_at`, and no `me` trigger touches `updated_at`) — filling the last axis would have left tracks stale-incomplete for the session and saved further bank-only packs; now refetched through a `refreshAfterAnswer` callback on `onUpdated`, deliberately *not* keyed on `trait_touched_at` since that is an object whose identity churns every fetch (the documented `checks`-identity trap); (2) the QuestionsFold mount is gated on `tracksReady`, or a pre-tracks mount would hand a complete-profile user a bank-only pack to work through; (3) the new draft-copy badge was added to `check:copy-review-badges`' pinned `sites` list, which then failed and exposed that an inline `style` prop between the gate and the badge text defeats that check's nearest-`{` heuristic — badge is now prop-free like the other eight sites. Known and documented: refreshed tracks land **one batch late** (`pick()` re-runs `load()` from a render closure holding the old prop), so the answer filling the last axis regenerates one more bank pack before the AI path opens — bounded, no quota spent. New copy ships behind `PROFILE_FILL_COPY_REVIEWED = false` (now nine such surfaces). `check:questions` 20→24, `check:traits` 29→32. Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks. **Not device-verified** — the fold owes the five-appearance-mode pass per `docs/GOTCHAS.md`; not yet OTA'd.

- 2026-09-03: **wave40 re-applied — `cat_structure`/`cat_resilience` are live in `category_defs` (11 rows total, 3 maps / 8 bars), confirmed after OTA `0d31f76d` was live on device.** Verified via SQL: both rows present with correct shape/axis_weights. Verified the render pipeline directly (ran the real `categoryDefFromRow` → `readCategory` → `fallbackForReading`/`categoryConcept` chain, not just a schema check): `cat_structure` on simulated high-openness/low-conscientiousness data correctly returns `map: { x: 0.85, y: 0.2 }` (not a bar) with the matching quadrant fallback line; `cat_resilience` on simulated high competence/growth_mindset/steadiness correctly returns `bar: 0.75` (not a map) with the matching band line; a `cat_structure` reading with only 1 of 2 axes settled correctly returns `ready: false`. This closes the `cat_structure`/`cat_resilience` rollout — both categories are now live end-to-end for real users. No on-device visual screenshot taken (native app, no browser preview available); the pipeline-level check above is the verification.

- 2026-09-03: **Published OTA `0d31f76d-a7a7-4b1e-8631-6dffa6d4dba7`** (commit `73ecba3`, runtime 1.0.0, ios+android, branch `production`) — ships the `cat_structure`/`cat_resilience` category code from the entry below. `npm run ota:publish` run after `check:ota-gate` confirmed green (typecheck + lint + 49 checks). **wave40 (`category_defs` DB seed) deliberately NOT re-applied yet** — waiting on confirmation this OTA is live on devices before re-inserting the two rows, per the ordering established below (client must ship before data, not after).

- 2026-09-03: **Two new profile categories added in code — `cat_structure` ("Structure vs. spontaneity") and `cat_resilience` ("Resilience under pressure") — DB seed reverted, OTA still pending.** Investigation found all 16 axes already had category coverage but 9 were single-category ("thin"); these two cover previously-unpaired thin-axis combinations without recreating an existing category's axis set. `cat_structure` is a **map** (x=openness, y=conscientiousness) rather than a bar — deliberate: the two axes trade off against each other in the onboarding scenario (`vibe-check.ts:24-31`, "new thing" vs "stick with plan"), so averaging them into one bar score would hide the tension exactly the way a bar would for Love/Independence, which is why those are maps too. `cat_resilience` is a bar (competence + growth_mindset + steadiness — all reinforcing, no trade-off). Added: `CategoryId` union + `CATEGORY_DEFS` entries (`categories.ts`), `CATEGORY_CONCEPTS` entries (`concept-explainers.ts`), `CATEGORY_FALLBACK_BANDS` + a new `MAP_QUADRANT_BANDS` table entry for `cat_structure` (`category-bands.ts`), `EXPLORE_CATEGORY_GROUNDING` entries (`explore/prompt.ts`) — all four are the complete set of `Record<CategoryId, …>`/exhaustive-union sites (confirmed by reviewer, no others in `src/`). Three existing check scripts updated for the new count (9→11 categories, 2→3 maps): `wave21-check.ts`, `wave22-check.ts`, `category-catalog-check.ts`. New migration `supabase/migrations/wave40_structure_resilience_categories.sql` (idempotent, `on conflict do update`, same shape as wave21/22). **Caught and corrected before push:** first pass inserted the two rows live via Supabase MCP immediately — reviewer flagged this as a live production bug, since `category-catalog.ts`'s 5-minute poll would have handed the two new ids to devices on the currently-shipped OTA (`229bc909`), which have no fallback-band/concept copy for them, crashing `CategoriesFold`/`fallbackBandFor` (`category-bands.ts` render path) for real users. **Rows deleted from production immediately** (confirmed back to 9) with emci's explicit go-ahead. Also fixed: one inverted band line (`cat_structure`'s high-score band read as low-openness), stale "9 categories"/"25 concept explainers" counts in `docs/MAP.md`/`docs/ME.md`/`docs/NOW.md`. **Left for emci to trigger, in order:** (1) confirm this commit is live via OTA on installed devices, (2) only then re-run/re-apply `wave40_structure_resilience_categories.sql` to re-seed the two rows — code and data must land in that order, not the reverse. Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks. Copy unreviewed (`CATEGORY_COPY_REVIEWED`/`CATEGORY_BAND_COPY_REVIEWED`/`CONCEPT_COPY_REVIEWED` all `false`, same as every other category).

- 2026-09-03: **Published OTA `229bc909-0954-445e-ba36-022188f2f34d` (commit `6f4623b`, production, ios+android).** Bundles the two preceding entries: Sage facts card moved Explore→Sage with the chat auto-scroll fix, the Legends thin-profile gate + Questions deep link, and the dev "Thin profile" preset. `npm run ota:publish` run only after `check:ota-gate` was confirmed green (typecheck + lint + 49 checks) — no additional changes made for this publish. **Not device-verified before publishing** — both prior decision entries explicitly flagged the Sage scroll fix and the Legends gate as code-verified only; emci chose to publish anyway rather than wait for a device pass. Supersedes `87207080`. Docs (NOW/ME/PROJECT_CONTEXT) synced with the group id. **Top open item: run the device pass against this specific OTA** (facts-card expand/collapse doesn't jump the thread; Legends thin-profile card + deep link lands on the right axis; `@atodev` Thin profile ↔ archetype round trip).

- 2026-09-03: **Dev "Thin profile" preset added — the repeatable way to reach the Legends thin-profile gate.** The 4 archetype presets are each built to *match* an archetype, so none could produce the gate's precondition (catalog non-empty, **no** archetype matched, `isThinProfile` true); testing it previously needed a throwaway account. New `applyDevThinProfilePreset()` (`dev-test-user.ts`) nulls all 16 `me` axis columns (so `countPoleHits` scores 0 and nothing matches) and upserts all 32 `trait_tracks` rows at `answer_count 0`, which `effectiveStability` floors to 0 → `settledCount` 0 → thin. **No migration needed:** wave20 grants only select/insert/update on `trait_tracks` and *explicitly revokes delete*, so rows are overwritten in place rather than removed — the `answer_count` floor makes that equivalent for every reader that gates on settled. A "Thin profile" chip on the Legends dev strip exposes it. **Two review criticals fixed before ship:** (1) the preset was **one-way** — `applyDevArchetypePreset` wrote only the `me` row, never tracks, so a single thin tap left the dev user permanently unsettled for Categories / Title / Story / Full Profile (all read settled tracks, not `me`), recoverable only by ~48 real answers; both presets now share a `devTracks()` helper and the archetype path restores tracks at `answer_count 3` / `stability 0.8` (clearing `STABILITY_FLOOR_N` and `TITLE_STABLE_MIN`), making the round trip real; (2) the Legends tracks effect keyed on `[me?.id, me?.updated_at]`, neither of which moves on a preset write, so tracks stayed stale and the gate would not have appeared at all — the strip now takes an `onApplied` callback that bumps `retryTick`, which the effect depends on. Also: archetype presets offset the **game** track by 0.3 on 3 axes so told-vs-played divergence stays testable (identical report/game values would have made `divergingAxesFromTracks` always empty); doc comment corrected to stop claiming this equals a brand-new account (a real new user has *zero* rows — this leaves 32 carrying a mid value, which Full Profile renders as filled mid-band axes). `check:dev-test-user` 14→16, including a reversibility assertion and a mid-profile match test. `docs/GOTCHAS.md` gained the `trait_tracks` delete-revoke trap and the preset-symmetry requirement. Gate green: typecheck + lint + 49 checks. **Not device-verified** — needs a dev build signed in as `@atodev`.

- 2026-09-03: **Axis-write-surface audit items 2–3 implemented (by DeepSeek), verified against code here, docs synced.** (1) **"What Sage remembers" moved Explore → Sage.** `SageFactsCard` (`sage-facts.tsx`) was a summary row that opened a full-screen `Modal`; it is now a self-contained collapsible card rendered below the 8-ball on the Sage tab (`sage.tsx`), with the `Modal`/`SafeAreaProvider`/`ScrollView` wrapper and the separate `SageFactsSheet` component deleted — same read/delete-only behavior, teaching still only from Chat's "Teach Sage this". `check:facts` inverted to assert Explore no longer references `SageFactsCard` and that the card sits after `<SageEightBall` on Sage. (2) **Legends thin-profile gate + deep link.** When the catalog is non-empty, no archetype matched, and `isThinProfile(settledCount(tracks))` is true, Legends shows "Your profile is still taking shape." with an "Answer questions" CTA that deep-links to `/intake-sweep?axis=<axis>`; `missingAxis()` picks the first fully-unanswered axis, else the least-settled report track. `intake-sweep.tsx` validates the `axis` param against `TRAIT_AXES` and passes it to `QuestionsFold` as the new optional `focusAxis` prop, which front-loads it ahead of the existing deferred axes in `priorityAxes` (deferral behavior otherwise unchanged). The generic empty copy dropped its now-redundant "once enough of your traits have settled" clause. **Verified this session:** no trait write path was touched — `self_scenario` stays in `DIRECT_TRAIT_SOURCES` and `self_game` in `INFERRED_TRAIT_SOURCES` (`traits.ts:48-59`), `trackKindForSource` still routes `self_game` to the game track, and `intake.ts` / `voice/library.ts` / `voice/nudge.ts` are untouched. **Item 1 of the audit (consolidating the separate axis write paths) was answered "stay separate" and is closed** — not attempted. **Five review findings fixed on top of DeepSeek's work before commit:** (a) **real regression** — the Sage toys render *inside* the chat thread's `ScrollView`, whose `onContentSizeChange` calls `scrollToEnd`, so expanding the facts card or deleting a fact would have yanked the user to the bottom of the conversation; added a `skipAutoScrollRef` in `sage.tsx` that the card trips via a new optional `onResize` prop, skipping exactly one auto-scroll (new messages still scroll normally); (b) `pending` (the "Forget this?" confirm, an *index*) was no longer reset on close after the modal's `useEffect` was dropped — a stale confirm could reopen against a different fact, now reset in the toggle handler; (c) the collapsed header's `accessibilityLabel` was only the count, hiding the title from VoiceOver — now `"<title>. <summary>"`; (d) the Legends tracks `.catch` never set `tracksReady`, so a fetch failure permanently suppressed the thin-profile card — now sets it (zero tracks reads as thin, the honest state); (e) dead `row ? effectiveStability(row) : 0` ternary removed (`effectiveStability(null)` already returns 0), and `facts-check.ts`'s stale "You-tab" labels renamed to "Sage-tab". Gate green after the fixes: typecheck + lint + 49 checks. Not yet OTA'd. **Known caveats, not fixed (pre-existing / out of scope):** if today's question pack is already open, `routeQuestions` returns the cached pack (`route.ts:157`) so the deep-linked axis waits for the next batch; `missingAxis`'s least-settled fallback can nominate an already-answered axis, which the prompt then labels "still unanswered"; and the two new copy lines ("Your profile is still taking shape." / "Answer a few questions so your traits settle…") have not had emci's copy read. **Device-verify the Sage facts card expand/collapse** — the scroll fix is code-verified only, the offline gate cannot catch it.

- 2026-09-03: **`loneliness` disruption chip now has its own nudge line + library domain entry** (completing the follow-up flagged in the intake-copy change). `library.md` gained a `## Loneliness` section (Cacioppo / Holt-Lunstad grounding, fence-clean "For Sage" block); `library.ts` maps `loneliness` in `LIBRARY_ENTRY_IDS`/`HEADING_TO_ID`/`KEYWORDS`/`knockDomainIds`; `nudge.ts` gained a `knockLine` case ("one small reach toward a real person — not a whole social life") plus `KNOCK_ALIASES`, and both files dropped the `part !== 'loneliness'` filter so the chip flows through. `content.generated.ts` regenerated via `npm run sync:voice`; `check:library` now asserts 7 domain entries + a loneliness selection test. Gate green (typecheck + lint + 49 checks). Not yet OTA'd.

- 2026-09-03: **Intake question copy + option values updated (onboarding + You-tab Settings), no schema/type rename.** `show_up` (single): "Under pressure, what's your instinct — push forward or pull back?" → Push through it / Stay steady / Go quiet / Push back / Reach out to someone. `talk_style` (single): "How do you come across when you talk to people?" → Loud / Even / Quiet. `knocks_you_off` (multi, You-label now **"Disruption"**): "What tends to drain your energy or throw off your schedule?" → Sleep / Money / Workload / People/conflict / Health / Loneliness (the old catch-all `something else` became the specific `loneliness`). `morning_cue` (single): "What's the first thing you actually do when you wake up?" → Make coffee / Get sunlight / Move my body / Check my phone / Sit in quiet (labels drop the "After I …" prefix; stored cue values stay infinitive).
