- 2026-09-03: **Fixed two bugs — edit-nav drag bar glitch and QR poster footer text.** (1) The "Edit navigation" drag list (`nav-edit-overlay.tsx`) used `Sortable.Flex` with no explicit layout, so it fell back to the library default `flexDirection: 'row'` + `flexWrap: 'wrap'` — a horizontal-wrapping layout for what is a vertical full-width list, so a dragged row was repositioned on wrong coordinates and glitched/disappeared instead of animating into its slot. Added `flexDirection="column" flexWrap="nowrap"`; the `onDragEnd → setDraftSlots` state wiring was already correct (per `react-native-sortables` docs, order state updates belong in `onDragEnd`, not `onOrderChange`). (2) The share poster footer (`share-poster.tsx`) read `ASTR//<handle>` and put "SCAN · ADD" beside the tag in one row; `scanTag` is now `ATO//<handle>` and `scanRow` is `flexDirection: 'column'` + `alignItems: 'flex-start'` so "SCAN · ADD" sits on its own line above `ATO//HANDLE`. Typecheck + `check:floor` (17/17) + `check:nav` (20/20) green. Drag fix is code-verified only — needs the on-device pass (`docs/ATO_DEVICE_TESTS.md`, "drag the grip → reorders live"). Not yet OTA'd.

- 2026-09-03: **Categories now load live from `category_defs` instead of the hardcoded `CATEGORY_DEFS` const.** New `src/lib/category-catalog.ts` fetches the table (RLS: `authenticated` select, wave21/22) on auth/me load and on `MeProvider.refresh()` (`force`), caches in-memory with a 5-minute TTL, and swaps it into `getCategoryDefs()` in `src/lib/categories.ts` — which every category-math function (`readAllCategories`/`readCategory`/`categoryById`/`categoriesFingerprint`/`readyCategories`/`categoriesTiedToAxes`/`categoryCopyClean`) and the direct `CATEGORY_DEFS` consumers (sage-title, sage-story, dawn-category, category-compare, circle, check-milestone-badge, CategoriesFold, CategoryTeaser) now read through. `CATEGORY_DEFS` stays the fallback (fetch failure or empty table) and is the only reliable source of map x/y axis order, since jsonb does not preserve key order (confirmed live: `cat_agency` weights come back scrambled). `categoryDefFromRow` maps `axis_weights`→`weights`, `min_axes_required_stable`→`minStable`, `texture_axes`→`texture`. New `check:category-catalog` (6/6) proves the seeded table reproduces the fallback exactly and that minStable/texture read the fetched values. Emci can now hot-edit category name/weights in the Supabase table editor — restart or pull-to-refresh shows it, no OTA. Gate green: typecheck + lint + wave21/22 + category-catalog.

- 2026-09-03: **Dropped the dead `me.recovery_style` column (wave39, applied live).** Nothing read or wrote it after the "Eight Quick Taps" removal (2026-09-01) — it was absent from the client Me type / IntakePatch / createMe, every prompt builder, and every Edge Function, and existed only as a frozen DB column plus a stale privacy-label line. `supabase/migrations/wave39_drop_recovery_style.sql` drops the column (and its `me_recovery_style_known` CHECK) and removes `p_recovery_style` from `complete_signup` (14→13 params, both branches, matching the live wave24 definition otherwise). Verified on production first that only the column + CHECK + `complete_signup` referenced it (no view/trigger/other function). Also removed `recovery_style` from the "Other User Content" privacy-label disclosure (`app-privacy-labels.md`) and the `DATA_AI_MAP.md` data-map row. Existing historical values discarded (nothing displayed them).

- 2026-09-03: **Confirmed `self_game`/game-track is internal-only (not dead), removed the dead `gameTracks` helper.** The game track is written on every gut-call answer and its value IS read — by `divergingAxesFromTracks` (`trait-history.ts:74`) for The Story's told-vs-played tension and Talk's divergence note — it just never renders as a standalone number (by design: gut-call never counts toward settled/Categories/Title). `gameTracks()` (`trait-stability.ts`) had zero call sites ever; removed it and added a comment documenting the game track as internal-only. No behavior change.

- 2026-09-03: **Fixed — "A faster pass" (`IntakeSweep`) was the only ungated AI surface (no consent, crisis, or quota check before the LLM call).** `routeQuestionSweep` (`src/lib/questions/sweep.ts`) now returns a `{ kind, drafts }` result and gates in the same order as Infinite Questions: consent → crisis → `claimQuestionsBatch()` (skipped on the local bank, which is still consent/crisis-gated) → generate. The component maps denied/pending/crisis/quota kinds to the existing `QUESTIONS_EMPTY_*` inline lines (no modal), and the tab screen now renders `<IntakeSweep>` only after `flagsReady` so a crisis cold-open can't claim+generate before the crisis flag lands. `check:questions` asserts the sweep gating. Gate green.

- 2026-09-03: **Fixed — "How you tend to move" showed a different trait number than the rest of Explore.** Root cause: `TraitBandsFold` read the raw `me.<axis>` column (damped by `mergeTraitWrite` for inferred sources), while Full Profile / Categories / Title / Story / the "settled" header all read the `trait_tracks` report EWMA, so the two folds disagreed for inferred (`self_situation`) answers. Fix: `filledTraitBands(row, tracks)` now reads the same `trackFor(tracks, axis, 'report')?.value ?? me.<axis>` source Full Profile uses (`src/lib/trait-bands.ts`), the fold takes a `tracks` prop, and `explore.tsx` passes its already-fetched tracks through — one canonical number per axis everywhere. No schema/data change. `check:trait-bands` updated to assert report-track-first + me fallback. Gate green.

- 2026-09-03: **Fixed — `self_scenario` trait answers silently failed to reach `trait_history` (wave38, applied live).** Root cause: the wave19 `trait_history_source_known` CHECK allowed 7 sources but omitted the client's `self_scenario` (a direct source added after wave19), so every `insertTraitHistory` batch containing a scenario-answer row aborted on the CHECK and was swallowed by the `.catch` in `persistMergedTraits` (`src/lib/me.ts`) while the `me` write itself succeeded. `supabase/migrations/wave38_trait_history_self_scenario.sql` drops and re-adds the CHECK with the full 8-value `TRAIT_SOURCES` list (self_scenario added; no other source was missing). **Applied to production via Supabase MCP and verified live**: the new constraint def lists `self_scenario`, and an authenticated dev-user `trait_history` INSERT with `source='self_scenario'` returned 201 (proof row deleted after). Two regression guards added so this drift class can't recur silently: a `wave19-check` assertion that wave38 retains every prior source, and `npm run check:trait-sources` (`scripts/trait-sources-check.ts`) — a parity guard asserting the latest migration's `trait_history_source_known` exactly equals the client `TRAIT_SOURCES` list (catches a future source added client-side without a migration). Gate green 48/48.

- 2026-09-03: **Bottom nav reworked to a 5-slot bar (wave37).** Slot 5 is always "More" (fixed, not draggable). Slots 1–4 always contain Home and Sage once each, in user-draggable positions; the other two slots hold pool tabs from an extensible registry (`NAV_TABS`: Explore / You / Questions / Around / Legends / Circle). Persistence moved from AsyncStorage (`ato.nav.order.v1`, dropped) to a new `me.nav_layout` jsonb column (`supabase/migrations/wave37_nav_layout.sql`, nullable → default). `src/lib/nav/nav-order.ts` replaced `NavOrder`/`homeFirst`/`MAIN_BAR_CAP` with `NavLayout`/`SLOT_COUNT`/`POOL_SLOTS` + `normalizeNavLayout`; `nav-context.tsx` now reads the layout from `useMeContext().me` and persists via `saveNavLayout` (`me.ts`); `app-tabs.tsx` renders the 5-slot bar; `nav-edit-overlay.tsx` is a 4-slot drag list + pool tray. Circle is a normal pool tab (still gated until a friend is scanned). Default on first load: Home / Explore / Sage / You / More. `check:nav` rewritten to the new model; `crisis-region-check.ts` stale `homeFirst` assertion fixed. Gate green. Not yet OTA'd; `wave37` not yet applied to production.

- 2026-09-03: **You-tab share-poster restyled to a "soft dark field + light scan plate" QR (Direction A, picked from visual samples).** The poster (`src/components/share-poster.tsx`) is cyberpunk-minimal without neon-on-black: field flips from cream `#FAF6EF` to muted charcoal `FIELD #1A1B20`, name/handle/caption re-toned (`FROST/MIST/CAPTION`), the QR sits on a warm light `PLATE #F6F2EA` with `QR_INK #191A1E` modules — **kept dark-on-light, `ecl="M"` made explicit (was the library default), `quietZone={8}`, same value/size as before → scan geometry byte-identical to the old ink-on-white code**, so scannability is unchanged. Added accent corner ticks (TL/BR inside the plate), a 9px mono `SCAN · ADD` / `ASTR//<HANDLE>` microcopy row (line-height pinned to 11 so it doesn't inherit ThemedText's 24px; width pinned to `qrSize` so a long handle truncates instead of widening the plate), and the caption "ATO" now takes the per-user accent instead of the fixed rose. Decorative-only items (per-user accent on FIELD/PLATE, e.g. indigo/yellow hues) are low-contrast by nature of the accent palette — accepted, nothing on the scan path. `scripts/floor-check.ts` now pins the new palette consts + `ecl="M"` instead of the removed `STEEL/BLOOM`. Gate green: typecheck + lint + 47 offline checks (`check:ota-gate`). **OTA'd Sep 3, 2026** (group `08ae69ca`, commit `74242a6`); a real-device scan test is still the recommended final check before calling it verified.

- 2026-09-03: **Closed a live handle→email enumeration leak.** `login_email_for_identifier` (`auth_password_optional.sql`) was granted EXECUTE to `anon` and `authenticated` and returned `auth.users.email` for any handle passed in — confirmed live on the `ato` project via direct SQL introspection (`pg_get_functiondef` + `information_schema.routine_privileges`) before touching anything. New `supabase/functions/password-login`: takes `{identifier, password}`, resolves the handle to an email with the service-role key (calling the same RPC server-side) and calls `signInWithPassword` itself — only the resulting session (or a generic `invalid_credentials`) goes back to the client; the email never does. `wave36_login_email_lockdown.sql` revokes the RPC's `anon`/`authenticated` EXECUTE grants, service_role only from here. `src/lib/auth-password.ts`'s `resolveLoginEmail` (fetched the email client-side) is replaced by `signInWithIdentifier` (calls the function, then `auth.setSession` with the returned tokens); `login.tsx` updated to match. `check:auth-password` (live) rewritten: asserts an anon RPC call is now rejected, and that the function's success/failure responses never contain an email. **Applied live in this same pass** — migration applied and function deployed to the `ato` Supabase project via MCP, with emci's explicit go-ahead for both the design (Edge Function over rate-limiting) and applying it immediately rather than leaving it for a manual follow-up.

- 2026-09-03: **Hidden dev-access unlock added; the old dev-test sign-in path removed entirely.** New `supabase/functions/dev-unlock` Edge Function: JWT required, checks a posted password against the `DEV_UNLOCK_PASSWORD` Supabase secret with a constant-time compare, and only ever answers a plain `{ ok: boolean }` — never unlocks on an empty or unset secret. Client: tapping the version number on You 7x (`src/components/dev-unlock-gate.tsx`) opens a password prompt; a correct answer sets `src/lib/dev-access-unlock.ts`'s in-memory flag (`useSyncExternalStore`, no AsyncStorage/SecureStore) for the rest of the app session only — a cold start always starts locked again. `canSeeDevLab` call sites (`dev-lab.tsx`, Home's dev row in `(tabs)/index.tsx`) now accept this flag alongside `PRE_LAUNCH_DEV`/`__DEV__`, root, and per-account grants. In the same pass, removed the "Sign in as dev user" button from `login.tsx`, the `__DEV__` cold-start auto-login from `use-session.ts`, and `DEV_TEST_EMAIL`/`DEV_TEST_PASSWORD`/`devTestAutoSignIn`/`isDevTestEmail` from `dev-test-user.ts` — that module now only holds the 4 Legends-archetype presets, which still require an already-signed-in `@atodev` session (normal sign-in: Apple/OTP/a password set in Settings). New `npm run check:dev-unlock` (7/7) guards the Edge Function contract, the in-memory-only store, and every gate call site; `check:dev-test-user` and `check:dev-lab-sections` updated to match. **Still needed before this works live:** `DEV_UNLOCK_PASSWORD` set as a Supabase secret and `dev-unlock` deployed (`supabase secrets set` / `supabase functions deploy` — no CI, so both are manual). Gate green apart from two pre-existing, environment-caused failures unrelated to this change (`typecheck` on a missing generated `.css.d.ts` for a web-only component; `check:legends-live` needing `EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`/`ANON_KEY`, not set in this sandbox).

- 2026-09-02: **Legends wave32 device-verify + first resurface seeded (wave33, data-only).** Verified the wave32 figure+variant client against production: the catalog loads 5 fact-checked variants from `legend_variants` with the `legend_figures` embed intact, and `buildLegendView` serves at most one variant per figure per batch (no repeat). Seeded Da Vinci's first resurface — a `v2` variant (migration `supabase/migrations/wave33_legend_variant_v2.sql`, applied live via Supabase MCP) on the same `figure_id` / `canonical_slug`, `variant_key='v2'`, linked to `arch_the_architect`, with a new angle (systematic self-directed study vs v1's never-finishing). - 2026-09-02: **Legends repeat policy changed — a figure can resurface via story variants (wave32, applied live).** Replaced the single-story `legends` table with a normalized `legend_figures` (the person/myth: `canonical_slug` unique, name, era, type) + `legend_variants` (the story: `figure_id` FK, `variant_key`, teaser, full_story, `fact_checked`; unique `(figure_id, variant_key)`). The 4 seeded legends migrated to figures + a `v1` variant each (variant UUIDs reuse the old legend ids, so `legend_archetypes` and `user_legend_history` FKs re-pointed with zero data rewrite; old `legends` table dropped). **Never-repeat is now per VARIANT** — `user_legend_history` unique `(user_id, legend_id)` where legend_id = variant id, so a figure can later resurface with a different angle (same archetype) or a different archetype it also fits. Client moved in the same pass: `store.ts` reads `legend_variants` (+ `legend_figures` embed), `match.ts` dedupes per figure (best unseen variant per figure per batch), `legend-card.tsx`/Legends tab consume `LegendVariant`, history writes log variant ids. Content-spec docs updated. Gate (`check:ota-gate`) green 44/44 before apply. **Migration applied to production 2026-09-02 via Supabase MCP `wave32_legend_variants`**; verified 4 figures/4 variants, all fact-checked, links intact. **Client live via OTA `c897410d` (commit `4b27a0b`, Sep 2, 2026).**

- 2026-09-01: Fixed — onboarding screen 0 (MBTI four-letter type-code chips) removed. It turned out to BE the "grid" screen inferring O/C/E/A; sliders on the following screens always overwrote those same axes, so it was dead weight plus an unnecessary trademark risk. Optional-intake screens renumbered 0-7 (was 0-8). `self_grid` trait-source token kept read-only for existing users' historical data.
- 2026-09-02: Replaced the entire optional-intake flow (5 sliders + 2 close-pattern + 1 disagreement screen) with 8 new forced-choice scenario questions, each covering exactly 2 of the 16 axes (all 16 covered once), source draft in `docs/optional-intake-2axis-scenarios-DRAFT.md`. Added a new direct trait source, `self_scenario` — tried `self_situation` (inferred) first per the original spec, but that damps every write toward 0.5 for a null prior, so all 16 answers would've landed permanently in the mid band and been invisible to Sage-knows/pole-lines/completeness; switched to a direct source after flagging it to emci. Also fixed a pre-existing `ChipGroup` layout bug (`intake-chips.tsx`, missing `flexShrink`/`maxWidth`) found via visual QA — long chip text was overflowing mobile viewports instead of wrapping.
- 2026-09-02: Audited the separate "Nine Quick Taps" core-intake screen (`intake.ts`/`core-intake-sweep.tsx` — unrelated to the scenario/axis flow above, none of its 9 fields ever wrote to a trait axis). Found `talk_style` already wired into the AI tone guide but too weak to notice; `evening_wind_down` and `current_focus` collected but with no guaranteed on-screen or push effect; `recovery_style` a true orphan. Fixed all four: strengthened `TALK_STYLE_GUIDE` (`voice/providers/types.ts`) with distinct word-choice/length/exclamation rules per tone; wired `evening_wind_down` into the evening Check push body via the existing `cueAfterYou` helper (`push-copy.ts`/`push.ts`/`push-runtime.tsx`) — a doc comment in `me.ts` had literally said "wiring later"; added a deterministic `current_focus` line to the Explore tab header; removed `recovery_style` entirely from the app (screen now "Eight Quick Taps", `CORE_INTAKE_TOTAL` 9→8). Mid-fix, review caught that the live `complete_signup` RPC (`wave23_optional_intake.sql`) overwrites `recovery_style` on re-save instead of coalescing (unlike every other field in the same function) — with the app no longer sending a value, any already-onboarded user hitting the re-save path would've had it silently nulled. Added `wave24_recovery_style_resave_coalesce.sql` to fix just that one line, confirmed with emci first; **applied to production 2026-09-02** (separate from Legends work). DB column itself untouched; existing users keep their historical value. Copy-quality review of the remaining 8 questions/chip labels drafted in `docs/eight-quick-taps-copy-review-DRAFT.md`, not yet applied — flags two prompts that informally say "nudge" for a feature that isn't the real in-app Nudge, plus a pre-existing first-person/second-person pronoun mismatch in cue chip values ("When you put my phone down") shared by morning_cue and evening_wind_down.
- 2026-09-02: Consolidated the 8-question optional-intake scenario flow (added earlier today) from a one-question-per-screen stepper onto a single scrollable page — new `OptionalIntakeSweep` in `optional-intake.tsx`, mirroring `CoreIntakeSweep`'s shape. `onboarding.tsx` now batches all 8 screens' writes into one `updateTraits` call instead of one per screen-advance (safe: all 8 questions share the `self_scenario` source and cover disjoint axes). `OptionalStep`/`OptionalGate`/`OptionalIntakeFill` (the separate You-tab fill-later surface) and `traits.ts`/`vibe-check.ts` untouched. Content and scoring unchanged — layout only. Note: answers now save once on "Save" rather than incrementally per question (same pattern `CoreIntakeSweep` already uses) — if a user answers some questions and leaves without saving, those answers are lost, unlike the old per-screen autosave.
- 2026-09-02: Fixed — "A faster pass" (You-tab → `IntakeSweep`/`intake-sweep.tsx`) stuck on "Loading…" forever. Its loading effect awaited `shouldUseLocalAi()` + `routeQuestionSweep()` with no timeout; a stalled AI call left `drafts` null permanently instead of falling through to the existing `setDrafts([])` fallback. Not caused by the same-day optional-intake single-page consolidation — that touched `onboarding.tsx` (which does render `IntakeSweep` at its `'sweep'` phase) but didn't change `IntakeSweep`'s own loading path. Fix: wrapped both calls in the app's existing `withTimeout` helper, 25s (routeQuestionSweep can make two sequential AI calls — draft + retry — so longer than the usual 15s single-call budget). Same missing-timeout pattern exists at `explore.tsx`/`explore-panel.tsx`/`questions-fold.tsx`/`dev-lab.tsx`, left unfixed (out of scope for this bug).
- 2026-09-02: Fixed — the AI questionnaire (`QuestionsFold`/`questions-fold.tsx`, the "Questions fold" screen) and `ExplorePanel`/`explore-panel.tsx` had the same missing-timeout bug just fixed in "A faster pass": `load()` awaited `routeQuestions`/`routeExplore` with no timeout, so a stalled AI call left the screen on "Loading…" forever. Fix: wrapped both calls in the existing `withTimeout` helper (25s), same pattern as `intake-sweep.tsx`; both files' existing catch blocks already fall back to the empty-state UI, unchanged. Model switcher (`resolveActiveProvider`) and API key/connection handling (client-side keys for Gemini, Supabase-edge-routed keys for Claude/Grok) were audited in the same pass and are correct — no bug there, errors are thrown/logged, never silently swapped to a wrong provider. Remaining known follow-up: `explore.tsx`/`dev-lab.tsx` still have the same unfixed pattern.
- 2026-09-02: Reorganized the Explore tab (`explore.tsx`) — it had grown into one long crammed list. "Observations" (`SageExploreObservations`, the daily reaction-tracked Sage insight feed, literally labeled "Observations" in the UI) was confirmed as the closest match to that name; the token-spend one-off insight (`SageInsightSpend`, "A closer look from Sage") is a related but separate feature. Grouped "Today's Read" (`SageTitleCard`), "A closer look from Sage" (`SageInsightSpend`), and "Observations" into the existing `SettingsFold` accordion already used by Categories and The Story, collapsed by default; the screen header stays always visible, not foldable. `SageTitleCard` is also rendered unwrapped on the profile screen (`full-profile-fold.tsx`), so its fold was placed at the Explore call site rather than inside the shared component, to avoid double-nesting folds there. Content/copy/data unchanged — layout only.
- 2026-09-02: Added a "Questions" nav tab and moved "A faster pass" (`IntakeSweep`/`intake-sweep.tsx`) into it. Registered `questions` in `NAV_TABS`/`ReorderableTabId` (`nav-order.ts`), defaulting into the More sheet alongside Around/Circle — per emci's call, since the main bar has a hard 5-icon cap (`nav-check.ts` enforces this), not the 6 a main-bar placement would need. Moved the screen from a root Stack route (`src/app/intake-sweep.tsx`, deleted) to a tab screen (`src/app/(tabs)/intake-sweep.tsx`, same `/intake-sweep` path — a separate, unrelated `/questions` route already existed from the "Tell Sage more" flow, so the tab keeps its own route name to avoid colliding with it). Removed "faster pass" from onboarding entirely — dropped the `'sweep'` phase from `onboarding.tsx`'s phase machine; it now completes straight to Home after the optional-intake step (or its skip). Removed the "A faster pass" row from the You tab's "tell Sage more" card; "Tell Sage more" → `/questions` is untouched. Mid-build, review caught `onDone` on the new tab screen was wired to a plain `refresh()` — since the shared `IntakeSweep` component's own "Done"/"Skip the rest" buttons call `onDone` to *exit* (not to save), that left both buttons doing nothing visible in the new tab context (no back-stack to pop, unlike the old root-stack screen). Fixed to navigate home (`router.replace('/')`). Also added the missing `NAV_PIXEL_HEADER_INSET` padding to `IntakeSweep`'s title row (every other tab screen has it; the live `NavPixel` sits fixed top-right over all tabs and would've overlapped the title) — safe to edit inside the shared component since onboarding's usage was the only other caller and that's now removed.
- 2026-09-02: Added a green/red connectivity dot to the hidden AI provider switcher (`ai-lab.tsx`, opened by 5-tapping the Build line). New `pingProvider()` (`src/lib/ai/generate.ts`) reuses the same internal dispatcher (`completeFor`) that `generateText` uses — same client-side path for Gemini/NVIDIA/Perplexity, same Supabase-edge `ai-generate` path for Claude/Grok — but lets errors propagate (generateText swallows them) and skips usage logging so pings don't inflate the self-tracked call counts on the same screen. New `ProviderStatusDot` component checks on mount, on provider switch, and every 60s, wrapped in the existing `withTimeout` (25s); tap-to-reveal error text substitutes for "hover" since this is a phone app (confirmed with emci). Confirmed quota-claiming (`claimAiCall`/`claimQuestionsBatch`) is caller-side only, never inside the generate layer, so pinging never spends a user's quota. Review caught two real bugs before ship: (1) switching providers kept showing the previous provider's green/red status for up to 25s while the new one was still checking — fixed by resetting to "Checking…" synchronously on provider change, while still avoiding flicker on the recurring 60s re-checks of the same provider; (2) the initial 4-token ping budget could make a healthy reasoning-model provider (Gemini's `thinkingLevel: low`, grok-3-mini) falsely show "Unavailable" because thinking tokens could consume the whole budget before any output text — raised to 64 tokens. Known residual risk (flagged, not blocking): 64 tokens still isn't a hard guarantee against every reasoning model's worst case, and a signed-out user will see Claude/Grok as red (edge function requires a JWT) even when they're healthy — both pre-existing/dev-only-screen tradeoffs, not regressions.
- 2026-09-02: `ai-lab.tsx` now shows a status row per provider (Claude/Gemini/Grok/NVIDIA/Perplexity) all at once on screen open, instead of only the active one — each row is its own `ProviderStatusDot` instance (`REMOTE_AI_PROVIDER_IDS.map(...)`), so all 5 ping in parallel on mount and each keeps its own independent 60s re-check, unchanged 25s timeout, still zero quota spend (`pingProvider` unchanged). Added a third status color: gray "No API key" (was "Not configured"), now reachable for Claude/Grok too, not just client-key providers. First review pass caught that this was dead code — `completeViaEdge` (`src/lib/ai/edge.ts`) was throwing the Supabase SDK's fixed generic `FunctionsHttpError.message` string, never the Edge Function's real JSON body (`{error:'claude_key_missing'}`), so the new `/key_missing/` match in `provider-status-dot.tsx` could never fire. Fixed by reading the unconsumed `Response` off `error.context` (`.json()`) inside `completeViaEdge` and throwing that body's `error` string instead when present. Duck-typed the SDK's `FunctionsHttpError` by `error.name`/`context` shape rather than importing the class, since `@supabase/functions-js` is only a transitive (undeclared) dependency of `@supabase/supabase-js` and this path (`completeViaEdge`) also runs in production `generateText`, not just the dev screen — avoids a silent regression risk if a future supabase-js upgrade ever restructures that sub-dependency. Not verified on-device (the dev screen sits behind auth, no test credentials available this session) — verified instead via `tsc --noEmit`, targeted `eslint`, and two reviewer passes tracing the exact runtime chain (Edge fn 503 → SDK `FunctionsHttpError` → unread body → dot status) against the installed `@supabase/functions-js` source.
- 2026-09-02: Diagnosed "AI responses seem broken" — no code fix, this was a config/billing gap across every provider, found by testing each one directly (curl + node scripts hitting the real Gemini API and the real edge function with the anon key) rather than through the app's auth-gated UI. **Gemini** (bundled default): `429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`, free-tier daily quota (20 req/day, `gemini-3.7-flash`) exhausted — confirmed twice, not transient. **Claude/Grok**: both `503`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`/`XAI_API_KEY` Supabase secrets unset (matches the known caveat above, now confirmed live not just theoretical). **NVIDIA/Perplexity**: never had client keys in `.env.local` to begin with — not regressions, just never provisioned. Net: every provider was already unusable before the connectivity-dot even existed; the dot just made the pre-existing gap visible. Nothing to fix in code; decisions needed from emci (Gemini plan/quota, the two missing secrets) are still open as of this writing.
- 2026-09-02: Added DeepSeek (`deepseek-v4-flash`, confirmed by emci against DeepSeek's own docs — not a model I could verify from training knowledge) as a 6th AI provider, same edge-routed pattern as Claude/Grok. New `completeDeepseek()` in `supabase/functions/ai-generate/index.ts` mirrors `completeGrok()` exactly (DeepSeek's API is OpenAI-compatible) — reads server-only Supabase secret `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` (503 `deepseek_key_missing` if unset), posts to `api.deepseek.com/chat/completions`. `deepseek` added to `AiProviderId`/`RemoteAiProviderId`/`PROVIDER_LIMITS`/edge-provider dispatch — `ai-lab.tsx` and `ProviderStatusDot` needed zero changes since both already iterate `REMOTE_AI_PROVIDER_IDS` generically. Deployed the edge function live and curl-verified the dispatch directly (`deepseek` → `503 deepseek_key_missing`, `claude` unchanged, an actually-bogus provider still `400`) — same verification technique as the diagnosis above, since the dev screen is still behind the auth wall this session couldn't get through. **Scope explicitly cut by emci this round**: usage/cost tracking (input/output tokens, estimated $ per call), a monthly spend cap on Gemini, and the auto-fallback-to-DeepSeek-on-cap behavior were all planned but deferred — DeepSeek exists as a provider Emci can manually switch to once `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` is set, nothing more. Known accepted limitation: `ai_provider_log`'s DB check constraint doesn't list `'deepseek'` yet, so real (non-ping) DeepSeek usage won't show up in the self-tracked call counts until that constraint is updated in a future schema change — fails silently (console-logged only), never blocks the actual AI response.
- 2026-09-02: Legends feature schema is complete and confirmed (design agreed this session, migration applied to production as `20260902164233_wave25_legends_archetypes`). New tables: `archetype_defs` (id, formal_name, slang_name, slang_definition, anime_flavor_tag, trait_axis, description), `legends` (canonical_slug unique dedup key, name, era_title, type historical|modern-deceased|mythical, teaser, full_story, fact_checked default false), `legend_archetypes` (junction), and `user_legend_history` (user-owned; unique on (user_id, legend_id) so a legend never repeats for a user across any batch, week_batch_id still stored for batch queries). `archetype_defs.trait_axis` is a comma-separated combo string validated by new `trait_axis_valid()` against the app's **16-axis** vocabulary with high|low poles — deliberately not Big Five, so archetypes can match real user trait data. Content tables are read-only for `authenticated` (RLS), history is owner select/insert only. Naming (`archetype_defs`) deliberately avoids confusion with the wave21 `category_defs` You-tab profile categories. All four tables live but **empty — content/seed data pending**, no client code consumes them yet; feature is schema-complete and confirmed, not product-shipped.
- 2026-09-02: Legends content v1 shipped — `archetype_defs` no longer empty (migration `20260902175327_wave26_legends_archetypes_content`). Two new card-flavor columns added: `throwback_voice` (e.g. Born to Lead) and `party_build` (e.g. Speedrunner). Seeded the first 12 archetypes (`arch_the_*`: Front-Liner, Watcher, Architect, Commander, Alchemist, Warm One, Chaos Agent, Steady One, Anchor, Disruptor, Wanderer, Believer), each with a 3-axis `trait_axis` combo hand-mapped from the 16-axis vocabulary (validated live by `trait_axis_valid()`), a one-line `slang_definition`, and a 2-sentence `description` in the app's friend voice. Three emci-driven remap passes tuned the combos: Watcher/Architect de-twinned from a shared openness+conscientiousness core to zero shared axes; Disruptor and Wanderer left/cut the crowded `conscientiousness:low` pool (Chaos Agent stays its only low-C companion); Alchemist and Warm One each dropped the axis they shared with Anchor (`steadiness:high`, `conflict_cooperativeness:high`) for `attachment_anxiety:low` / `attachment_avoidance:low` — **no pair of archetypes shares more than one axis-pole** and no combo duplicates. ## Audit — Explore / Questions / token spend (2026-09-04, read-only)

Requested by emci as a read-only audit before any Explore↔Questions work. No code changed. All line numbers verified against the working tree at commit `9fe49ff`.

### 1) Explore's trait/category data model

**16 trait axes** — `TRAIT_AXES` in `src/lib/traits.ts:16-33`, with `export type TraitAxis = (typeof TRAIT_AXES)[number]` at `traits.ts:35`. This is the single source of truth for axes. The list:
`openness, conscientiousness, extraversion, agreeableness, steadiness, attachment_anxiety, attachment_avoidance, conflict_assertiveness, conflict_cooperativeness, autonomy, competence, relatedness, growth_mindset, locus_of_control, self_efficacy, playfulness`
`EXTRA_AXES` (`traits.ts:37-45`) is the 7 non-Big-5 axes. Axis *definitions* are TS-only; axis *values* are per-axis numeric columns on the `me` row, read by `traitStateFromRow` (`traits.ts:215-243`) alongside jsonb `trait_sources` / `trait_touched_at`.

**Categories** — `CategoryId` union in `src/lib/categories.ts:18-29`, defs in `categories.ts:45-145`. The code currently ships **11** category ids, not 9:
`cat_steadiness, cat_openness, cat_drive, cat_agency, cat_social, cat_communication, cat_love, cat_independence, cat_levity, cat_structure, cat_resilience`
Levity is `categories.ts:118-126` (axes: `playfulness, conflict_assertiveness, conflict_cooperativeness`). `CATEGORY_COPY_REVIEWED = false` (`categories.ts:16`) — still unreviewed copy.
Categories are a **hybrid** source: the TS `CATEGORY_DEFS` const is the seed/fallback, and a live `category_defs` DB table can override it via `categoryDefFromRow` (`categories.ts:214-247`) + `setCategoryDefs`/`getCategoryDefs` (`categories.ts:152-165`). All category math reads `getCategoryDefs()`, never the const directly (see the comment at `categories.ts:147-151`).

### 2) Questions' data model — typed, not free-text

`src/lib/questions/types.ts` imports `TraitAxis` from `@/lib/traits` (`types.ts:2`) and types `axis: TraitAxis` on both `QuestionDraft` (`types.ts:13-17`) and `QuestionItemRow` (`types.ts:19-28`). So a question references the **same source of truth as Explore**, not a loose string name. There is no `category_id` on a question at all.

The one untyped entry point — model-generated JSON — is validated: `isAxis()` (`src/lib/questions/parse.ts:10-12`) checks `TRAIT_AXES.includes(value)`, and `parseQuestionDraft` (`parse.ts:23-33`) returns `null` for an unknown axis. Both `parseQuestionBatch` and `parseQuestionSweep` route through it. **No bypass found.** The static bank (`src/lib/questions/bank.ts:8-33` onward) uses axis literals that typecheck against the union.

### 3) Question engine coverage vs Explore

- **Explore's units:** 16 trait axes **and** 11 category ids.
- **Question engine's units:** 16 trait axes only. `src/lib/questions/*` never imports `CategoryId` or `categoryById`.

Diff:
- All 16 axes are covered — the bank has ≥1 item per axis, `parseQuestionSweep` caps at `TRAIT_AXES.length` (`parse.ts:78`), and `unfilledAxes`/`priorityAxes` drive axis rotation (`route.ts:186-192`). **No missing axis.**
- **All 11 categories have zero coverage** — the engine cannot target or generate toward a category. This is consistent with the architecture (questions write axes; categories are a read-side aggregation of axes), but it is **not documented anywhere as a deliberate non-goal**, so it reads as a gap. If "ask questions to complete category X" is ever wanted, it needs a new axis-mapping layer.
- Nothing the engine targets is unknown to Explore — the engine's set is a strict subset.

### 4) Token/credit spend sites and completeness gates

Server-side spend funnels through `supabase/functions/ai-generate/index.ts:329` — `caller.rpc('claim_ai_call', ...)` fires **before** any vendor call; `callType` is only `'explore'` or `'sage'` (`index.ts:326`), so the DB layer cannot tell most features apart.

| Surface | File:line | Completeness gate | Blocks the paid call? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Infinite Questions batch | `questions/route.ts:168-175` (→ `questions/generate.ts:8-13`) | `isProfileComplete(tracks)` | **Yes** — falls back to the static bank, no `claimBatch`, no model call |
| Sage Story | `sage-story-fold.tsx:66` gate, `:84` claim (`claim_story_generate`) | `storyReady()` = `!isThinProfile(...)` | **Yes** — blocks quota claim and generation |
| Sage insight (on demand) | `sage-insight.ts:22,51,56` | `isThinProfile` | No — **copy only**, call still fires |
| Daily card | `voice/providers/remote.ts:18-23`, prompt at `prompt.ts:189-191` | `isThinProfile` | No — **copy only** |
| Talk reply (Sage chat) | `remote.ts:33-38` | none (explicitly ungated, see `questions/route.ts:167`) | No |
| Explore observations | `explore/route.ts:103-104` claim, `explore/generate.ts:12` | **none** — quota check only | No |
| Sage Title ("Today's Read") | `sage-title-card.tsx:89` claim, `:94` generate | **none** — quota check only | No |

### Gaps flagged (no fix proposed yet)

1. **Explore observations and Sage Title can spend a paid call on an empty or thin profile** — they check quota but never completeness, unlike Questions and Story. These are the two surfaces most likely to burn credits producing nothing useful for a brand-new user.
2. **"Copy-only" gates read like protection but aren't** — Daily card, Talk, and Sage insight call `isThinProfile` and then still spend. Worth being explicit about which of these is intentional.
3. **No category-level question targeting**, and no note anywhere saying that's on purpose.
4. **`CategoryId` has 11 members** while the brief and some docs say 9 — `docs/NOW.md` / `docs/ME.md` should be reconciled with the code, or the extra two (`cat_structure`, `cat_resilience`) confirmed as intended.
5. `claim_ai_call` only distinguishes `explore` vs `sage`, so per-surface spend cannot be attributed from the DB alone.

### Audit follow-up — "leaning" string, category source of truth, question coverage (2026-09-04, read-only)

Second read-only pass requested by emci. No code changed.

**1) The exact string "how are you leaning" does not exist in the codebase.** The closest matches are a different word order, `how you are leaning`, and none of them is a question, a trait, or a category — all are **UI copy**:
- `src/components/full-profile-fold.tsx:215` and `:220` — `'Update how you are leaning'`, the accessibility label and button text on a Full Profile row.
- `src/lib/full-profile.ts:18` — the disclaimer `'This can change. It is how you are leaning right now — not a type, not a diagnosis.'`
- `src/lib/full-profile.ts:16` — the related section label `FULL_PROFILE_LABEL = "How you're currently leaning"` (this is the Explore-tab section title, cited in `docs/NOW.md` and `docs/ME.md`).

So: **copy for the Full Profile fold, not a data-model entity.** If emci saw this phrasing in the app and expected a question behind it, there isn't one — the row it labels edits an existing trait axis value directly.

**2) Category source of truth — live `category_defs` table matches the TS seed exactly.** Queried production (`aijzsmupaaaxjctfgwpl`) this pass: **11 rows**, same ids, names, shapes, axes, and `min_axes_required_stable: 2` as `CATEGORY_DEFS` in `src/lib/categories.ts:45-145`. No drift, no extra or missing rows, nothing overriding. (DB column names differ from the TS field names: `axis_weights` jsonb, `min_axes_required_stable`, `texture_axes`.)

| # | ID | Name | Shape | Axes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `cat_steadiness` | Steadiness | bar | conscientiousness, agreeableness, steadiness |
| 2 | `cat_openness` | Openness to life | bar | openness, extraversion |
| 3 | `cat_drive` | Drive | bar | autonomy, competence, relatedness |
| 4 | `cat_agency` | Agency | bar | growth_mindset, locus_of_control, self_efficacy |
| 5 | `cat_social` | Everyday social energy | bar | extraversion, agreeableness, playfulness |
| 6 | `cat_communication` | Communication | bar | conflict_assertiveness, conflict_cooperativeness |
| 7 | `cat_love` | Love / closeness | map | attachment_anxiety, attachment_avoidance (+texture: conflict_assertiveness, conflict_cooperativeness) |
| 8 | `cat_independence` | Independence & closeness | map | autonomy, relatedness |
| 9 | `cat_levity` | Levity | bar | playfulness, conflict_assertiveness, conflict_cooperativeness |
| 10 | `cat_structure` | Structure vs. spontaneity | map | openness, conscientiousness |
| 11 | `cat_resilience` | Resilience under pressure | bar | competence, growth_mindset, steadiness |

**3) Questions tagged to a category: none, for any category.** Confirmed again this pass — `grep -rn "cat_\|CategoryId\|categor" src/lib/questions/` returns **zero matches**. A question carries only `axis: TraitAxis`; there is no category field anywhere in the question schema, the bank, the prompt, or the DB rows.

The static bank (`src/lib/questions/bank.ts`) holds **exactly 16 items — one per axis**, and `local.ts:20-21` de-dupes to one row per axis, so there is no second item for any axis. The AI generator can emit any of the 16 axes but likewise never a category.

Table below reads: *tagged directly to the category* = always no; *reachable* = how many bank questions sit on axes this category is computed from (i.e. how many the category indirectly depends on).

| Category | Has questions tagged to it | Count (tagged) | Bank questions on its axes (indirect) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cat_steadiness` | No | 0 | 3 |
| `cat_openness` | No | 0 | 2 |
| `cat_drive` | No | 0 | 3 |
| `cat_agency` | No | 0 | 3 |
| `cat_social` | No | 0 | 3 |
| `cat_communication` | No | 0 | 2 |
| `cat_love` | No | 0 | 2 (+2 texture, not in the math) |
| `cat_independence` | No | 0 | 2 |
| `cat_levity` | No | 0 | 3 |
| `cat_structure` | No | 0 | 2 |
| `cat_resilience` | No | 0 | 3 |

**What this means:** every category is fed indirectly, and every one of the 16 axes is covered by at least one bank question, so no category is unreachable. But nothing in the system can currently answer "ask me questions until Levity is ready" — that would need a new category→axis targeting layer, since `priorityAxes` (`questions/route.ts:186-192`) only accepts axes. Also worth noting the bank is **one question per axis total**; the depth all comes from the AI generator, so with the model unavailable a user sees the same 16 fallback questions and nothing more.

### Audit follow-up 2 — the three Explore counters + full app-wide token spend map (2026-09-04, read-only)

Third read-only pass. No code changed.

#### 1) The three counters — three *different* predicates over the same data

All three read the **report track only**; the `self_game` gut-call track never counts toward any of them (`trait-stability.ts:127-134`).

**a) "Full profile · X of 16 filled"** — [profile-fill-fold.tsx:38](src/components/profile-fill-fold.tsx:38), title built from `filledAxisLabel(tracks)`.
Chain: `filledAxisLabel` (`trait-stability.ts:239`) → `filledCount` (`:218`) → `filledAxes` (`:210`) → `isAxisFilled` (`:206`) = **`row.answerCount >= 1`**. That is the whole test — one answer on an axis fills it. At 16/16, `isProfileComplete` (`:233`) flips the title to the complete label instead.

**b) "How you're currently leaning · X of 16 settled"** — [explore.tsx:144](src/app/(tabs)/explore.tsx:144) (label) and `:173` (number), from `settledAxisLabel` / `settledCount`.
Chain: `settledCount` (`trait-stability.ts:156`) = `Math.round(settledScore)`; `settledScore` (`:148`) sums `effectiveStability(...)` across all 16 axes. `effectiveStability` (`:114`) returns **0 when `answerCount < STABILITY_FLOOR_N` (3)**, else `decayedStability` — full value for 60 idle days, then a 90-day half-life (`:102-112`).
So "settled" is a **rounded sum of fractional stabilities, not a count of axes**. It can move without any new answer (decay), and 16 axes each at 0.5 stability reads as "8 of 16 settled" while every one of them is filled. Filled and settled are documented as allowed to disagree (`trait-stability.ts:197-202`, `profile-fill-fold.tsx:30-32`).

**c) "Categories · X of 11 ready"** — [categories-fold.tsx:76](src/components/categories-fold.tsx:76), literally `${ready.length} of ${readings.length} ready` where `ready = readings.filter((row) => row.ready)` (`:40`).
`ready` comes from `readCategory` (`categories.ts:294-345`). **What "ready" means depends on the category's shape:**
- **bar** (8 of 11): `ready = stable.length >= def.minStable`, and `minStable` is **2** for every category — so a 3-axis bar is "ready" on 2 of its 3 axes.
- **map** (3 of 11 — `cat_love`, `cat_independence`, `cat_structure`): **both** named axes must independently clear the floor, else it does not render (`categories.ts:309-317`).
- "Clears the floor" = `reportStable` (`categories.ts:266-274`) → `effectiveStability(...) > 0` → **`answerCount >= 3` on that axis**, after decay.
- `texture` axes (only `cat_love` has them) are display-only and never affect `ready`.

**Net:** an axis needs **1** answer to count as filled, **3** to contribute to settled or to a category. A category needs 2 qualifying axes. These thresholds are independent, which is why the three numbers on one screen routinely disagree.

#### 2 & 3) Every AI-token-spending call site in the app, and what it checks

Spend is claimed in two places: client-side RPCs (`claimAiCall`, `claimQuestionsBatch`, `claimTitleGenerate`, `claimStoryGenerate`) and, for edge-routed providers, `claim_ai_call` inside the Edge Function itself (`supabase/functions/ai-generate/index.ts:329`) — which fires **before any vendor call and regardless of what the client checked** (`edge.ts:21-22` notes the client must not double-claim).

| # | Surface | Call site | Quota claim | Checks *filled* (`isProfileComplete`) | Checks *settled* (`isThinProfile`) | Checks *categories ready* | Blocks spend? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Daily card (Dawn / Home / missed-check) | `voice/providers/remote.ts:18-23`, via `routeVoiceCard` (`voice/router.ts:283`) — callers `(tabs)/index.tsx:41`, `dawn.tsx:22`, `missed-check-card.tsx:11` | Edge-side only | No | Yes — `prompt.ts:189-191`, **copy only** | No | **No** |
| 2 | Talk reply (Sage chat) | `remote.ts:33-38`, via `voice/talk.ts:134` (`(tabs)/sage.tsx:370`) | `claimAiCall('sage')` | No — explicitly ungated (`questions/route.ts:167`) | No | No | **No** |
| 3 | Explore observations (Explore tab) | `(tabs)/explore.tsx:295-298` → `explore/route.ts:103-104` | `claimAiCall('explore')` | No | No | No | **No** |
| 4 | Explore observations (panel) | `explore-panel.tsx:145-148` → same route | `claimAiCall('explore')` | No | No | No | **No** |
| 5 | Sage Title ("Today's Read") | `sage-title-card.tsx:89` claim, `:94` generate | `claim_title_generate` | No | No | No | **No** |
| 6 | Sage insight ("A closer look") | `sage-insight.ts:56` | via `claimAiCall` at caller | No | Yes — `sage-insight.ts:22,51`, **copy only** | No | **No** |
| 7 | Infinite Questions batch | `questions/generate.ts:8` via `questions/route.ts:168-175` (`questions-fold.tsx:138`) | `claim_questions_batch` | **Yes** — `isProfileComplete` | No | No | **Yes** — falls back to the 16-item static bank, no claim, no model call |
| 8 | The Story | `sage-story-fold.tsx:66` gate → `:84` claim | `claim_story_generate` (own lane) | No | **Yes** — `storyReady()` = `!isThinProfile(...)` | Reads `readyCategories(tracks)` at `:110`, but only to **fill the prompt**, not to gate | **Yes** |
| 9 | Dev Lab explore probe | `dev-lab.tsx:267-270` | `claimAiCall('explore')` | No | No | No | No (dev-only screen) |
| 10 | Dev Lab card probe | `dev-lab.tsx:97` → `routeVoiceCard` | Edge-side only | No | No | No | No (dev-only screen) |
| 11 | Voice Lab card probe | `voice-lab.tsx:11` → `routeVoiceCard` | Edge-side only | No | No | No | No (dev-only screen) |
| — | Provider ping (`ai-lab.tsx`) | `pingProvider()` (`ai/generate.ts`) | **none by design** — never claims quota | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a |

**Summary of the completeness picture:**
- **Only 2 of 8 production spend sites block on completeness**: Infinite Questions (`filled`) and The Story (`settled`).
- **Two more look guarded but aren't** — the Daily card and Sage insight call `isThinProfile` and then spend anyway; it only softens the wording.
- **"Categories ready" gates nothing anywhere in the app.** `readyCategories` has exactly one consumer (`sage-story-fold.tsx:110`) and it is a prompt input, not a gate.
- The two gates that do exist use **different signals** (`isProfileComplete` on filled vs `isThinProfile` on settled), so a user can be blocked from one surface and allowed on the other with the same profile.

## Research pass — question inventory, skip routing, authoring format, unlock UI (Sep 4, 2026)

Read-only. No code changed. Context: the `isProfileSettled` gate (Decisions log, same day)
means every AI surface now depends on all 16 axes reaching 3+ consistent answers, so the
question supply is the bottleneck.

### 1) Static questions per axis and per category — the 1-per-axis floor is EXACTLY met, with zero slack

`QUESTIONS_BANK` ([src/lib/questions/bank.ts:7](src/lib/questions/bank.ts:7)) is **16 items for 16 axes — exactly one each, no axis missing, no axis doubled.**

| | |
|---|---|
| Bank size | 16 |
| Axes covered | 16 of 16 (`TRAIT_AXES`) |
| Axes with 0 questions | none |
| Axes with >1 question | **none** |
| Option counts | 3 options on 13 items; 2 options on 3 (`attachment_avoidance`, `relatedness`, `growth_mindset`) |

Per category ([CATEGORY_DEFS](src/lib/categories.ts:47)) — "total qs" is just the sum of its axes' questions, so it equals the axis count:

| Category | Shape | Axes | Total static qs | Ready needs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| cat_steadiness | bar | 3 | 3 | 2 stable |
| cat_openness | bar | 2 | 2 | 2 stable |
| cat_drive | bar | 3 | 3 | 2 stable |
| cat_agency | bar | 3 | 3 | 2 stable |
| cat_social | bar | 3 | 3 | 2 stable |
| cat_communication | bar | 2 | 2 | 2 stable |
| cat_love | map | 2 | 2 | **both** |
| cat_independence | map | 2 | 2 | **both** |
| cat_levity | bar | 3 | 3 | 2 stable |
| cat_structure | map | 2 | 2 | **both** |
| cat_resilience | bar | 3 | 3 | 2 stable |

**SUPERSEDED the same day** (see the two Decisions log entries for 2026-09-04 on the bank expansion and the variant selector): the bank is now 3 drafts per axis, `bankByAxis` keeps all of them, and `axisVariant` serves a different one per answer — so the "can never settle from static content" conclusion below no longer holds. Kept as the dated finding that motivated the fix.

**The floor is met but it is only a floor.** The bank is a *fallback*, not a supply: `composeLocalSweep` / `bankByAxis` ([local.ts:18](src/lib/questions/local.ts:18)) dedupe to the **first** draft per axis, so a second bank question on an axis would be ignored by the sweep as written. And `effectiveStability` needs `answerCount >= 3` per axis — with one static question per axis, **the static bank alone can never settle a single axis.** Reaching `isProfileSettled` today requires the Gemini-generated `routeQuestions` batches, the gut-call/ranking decks, or repeat passes. Worth deciding explicitly: the gate just shipped is not reachable from static content alone.

### 2) What intake does on skip — it defers, it does not drop the user

Two separate skip paths, both non-destructive:

- **Full sweep** (`IntakeSweep`, [intake-sweep.tsx:141](src/components/intake-sweep.tsx:141)): "Skip this one" / "Skip the rest" call `persistSkip` -> `mergedDeferral` -> `saveQuestionDeferral`, writing the axis onto **`me.question_deferred`** (jsonb array). An axis is only ever "deferred *while unanswered*" — `deferredUnansweredAxes` drops it the moment it gains a trait value ([deferral.ts:28](src/lib/questions/deferral.ts:28)).
- **Rotating Questions** (`QuestionsFold`, [questions-fold.tsx:202](src/components/questions-fold.tsx:202)): "Skip this one" -> `skipQuestionItem` (per-item `skipped_at`); "Skip the rest" -> `skipRestOfQuestionPack`, result kind `paused`. There is also a checkpoint after 8 (`QUESTIONS_CHECKPOINT_AFTER`).

**Where a skipped axis goes:** back into the *regular rotating pool*, not into limbo. `questions-fold.tsx:117-132` reads `deferredUnansweredAxes` and passes them as `priorityAxes` to `routeQuestions`, which front-loads them in the next batch ([route.ts:186-192](src/lib/questions/route.ts:186)). So skip = "ask me later, in the other surface", which is the right behaviour for the new gate.

**Two caveats:**
- Only **"Skip the rest"** on the full sweep navigates (`onDone()` -> `router.replace('/')`, [intake-sweep.tsx:203](src/app/(tabs)/intake-sweep.tsx:203)) — it drops the user to **Home**, not anywhere profile-related. Skipping one stays in place.
- `routeQuestionSweep` ([sweep.ts:41](src/lib/questions/sweep.ts:41)) deliberately has **no** completeness gate ("gating it would make it unreachable exactly when it is most useful") and claims no quota — it is pure local bank. That is the one path a locked user can always reach, which matters now.

### 3) Adding MC / true-false variants per axis — **content change, not schema change**

The storage is already shape-agnostic:

- Type is `{ axis, prompt, options: { text, value }[] }` ([types.ts:13](src/lib/questions/types.ts:13)) — `value` is a free 0..1 number, so a 2-option true/false is just `[{text:'True',value:0.8},{text:'False',value:0.2}]`. **Three bank items are already 2-option**, so TF is an existing shape, not a new one.
- DB: `question_items.options` is **`jsonb`**, unconstrained in the column ([wave17:189](supabase/migrations/wave17_infinite_questions.sql:189)). `axis` has a 16-value CHECK (`question_items_axis_known`); `prompt` is capped at 400 chars.

**Three real constraints to design within — all are edits, none are migrations:**
1. **Option count is hard-capped at 2-3 in three places:** the `insert_question_pack` RPC raises "question needs 2-3 options" ([wave17:250](supabase/migrations/wave17_infinite_questions.sql:250)), `parseQuestionDraft` rejects outside 2..3 ([parse.ts:31](src/lib/questions/parse.ts:31)), and the prompt says "2 or 3 options each" ([prompt.ts:63](src/lib/questions/prompt.ts:63)). A 4-option MC **would** be a schema change (the RPC). 2-3 is not.
2. **`sort_index` CHECK is `>= 0 and < 5`** ([wave17:180](supabase/migrations/wave17_infinite_questions.sql:180)) — a persisted pack can never exceed 5 items. Not a blocker for the 16-item sweep, which is local-only and writes traits directly via `updateTraits`, never through `insert_question_pack`.
3. **`bankByAxis` keeps only the first draft per axis** ([local.ts:18](src/lib/questions/local.ts:18)) — adding a 2nd/3rd variant per axis needs a selection change there (rotation/random pick), otherwise the extra variants are dead content. This is the one code change a "variants" feature actually requires.

Also note `INTAKE_SWEEP_COPY_REVIEWED = false` ([local.ts:16](src/lib/questions/local.ts:16)) — new question copy ships behind the unreviewed badge until emci reads it, same discipline as crisis copy.

### 4) Existing "unlock / congrats" UI worth reusing for a "full profile unlocked" moment

There is a **complete, working unlock vocabulary already built** — and one of its badges is already almost exactly this moment:

- **`MilestoneBadges`** ([check-milestone-badge.tsx:172](src/components/check-milestone-badge.tsx:172)) — collapsible strip of chips, each `{ kicker, value, unlocked }`. Explicitly documented as "**Not a popup**". Glow only once true (`presenceGlowLayersForTier`, `chipShadow`), locked chips dimmed via `styles.locked`.
- **`full-picture` capstone badge** ([badges.ts:9](src/lib/badges.ts:9), copy at [check-milestone-badge.tsx:159](src/components/check-milestone-badge.tsx:159)) — the closest existing analogue. Rendered with `capstone` styling (accent fill, 2px border, `onAccent` text) instead of a plain chip, and when locked its `onPress` deep-links to `/intake-sweep?axis=<leastReadyAxis>` — **the exact CTA pattern the new lock states use.** It currently fires on `allCategoriesReady(tracks)`, which is a *different* predicate from `isProfileSettled`; worth deciding whether they should be one badge or two.
- **`ProfileFillFold`** ([profile-fill-fold.tsx:35](src/components/profile-fill-fold.tsx:35)) — swaps its own title/lede to `PROFILE_FILL_COMPLETE_LABEL` ("Complete") / `_LEDE` at 16/16. A title-swap-on-completion pattern, no ceremony.
- **`AppearancePicker`** ([appearance-picker.tsx:42](src/components/appearance-picker.tsx:42)) — locked rows stay visible with a dimmed swatch + a small badge, rather than disappearing. Good precedent for "show the thing you'll get".
- **`NotedAck`** ([explore.tsx](src/app/(tabs)/explore.tsx), [explore-panel.tsx](src/components/explore-panel.tsx)) — the only celebratory *animation* in the app: a reanimated `withSequence` fade with a `reduceMotion` branch. Reusable if a moment should be more than a state change.

**Gap to note:** there is **no first-time-unlock detection anywhere** — no `seen_*` / `hasSeen` state in `src/lib`. Every badge is a pure function of current data, so it renders identically on the 1st and 100th view. A true "you just unlocked your full profile" *moment* (fires once, then stops) would need new persisted state; a permanently-glowing capstone chip would not.

The Legends-related migrations (`wave25_legends_archetypes.sql`, `wave26_legends_archetypes_content.sql`, `wave28_legends_approved_seed.sql`, `wave29_legends_fact_check.sql`, `wave32_legend_variants.sql`, `wave33_legend_variant_v2.sql`, `wave37_nav_layout.sql`) are static schema/seed SQL with no edge-function or HTTP calls; the one keyword hit (`wave29_legends_fact_check.sql:7`) is a comment about hedging a historical anecdote in seed copy, not code. - 2026-09-04: **Audit, read-only, follow-up: whole-repo sweep for dormant/planned Legend-generation code finds none live — only a documented future plan and unrelated "weekly batch" serving infra.** Broadened from the prior Legends-only audit to the full repo (33 files match "legend" case-insensitively; the 8 already covered plus 25 more — docs, scripts, migrations). The future weekly-batch AI job generates to this exact format and no other"* — a content-format spec written so a not-yet-built generator could target it later; describes exact teaser/story structure (opening = teaser restated, one grounding paragraph, blank line, "[Archetype] Energy:" close) and gives the 4 approved reference legends verbatim.
